{"parentUuid":"7a52b687-d98a-4c14-b859-0ff47153eea4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RZTTA4UARw3XLe4SKYu2oB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tid: war-room-dedicated-page\n3\ttitle: \"War Room as a dedicated sub-page with session list, scoping, and historical view\"\n4\tepic: enhanced-logging\n5\tstatus: ready-for-dev\n6\tsecurity_review: required\n7\tmatrix: false\n8\tui: true\n9\tcreated: 2026-07-06\n10\t---\n11\t\n12\t# War Room as a Dedicated Sub-Page\n13\t\n14\t## Story\n15\t\n16\t**As** a Nebu platform operator,\n17\t**I want** a dedicated `/admin/logs/war-room` page that lists active and historical War Room sessions with full session details, and the ability to drill down into a session to view all logs captured by that session, rather than accessing War Room only as an embedded panel on `/admin/logs`,\n18\t**so that** I can discover, review, and investigate diagnostic sessions without searching through a crowded monitoring page, pre-scope a session before starting it to avoid capturing unwanted high-frequency traffic, and maintain a permanent audit trail of which sessions captured what and when.\n19\t\n20\t## Background\n21\t\n22\tThe `war-room-mode` story (delivered) implemented War Room as an embedded inline panel on `/admin/logs`: a form with `max_entries`/`max_duration_s` inputs and a \"🚨 Start War Room\" button, backed by the `war_room_sessions` table and `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` API. The operator who requested this feature reviewed the shipped version in production and found two usability gaps:\n23\t\n24\t1. **Discoverability and session review**: The panel is cramped on an already-full monitoring page. Operators need a proper list view of all sessions (active + historical ended) to review which sessions exist, who started them, what scope/filters were applied, and when. This also creates an audit trail that survives the next monitoring-page reload or admin logout.\n25\t\n26\t2. **Pre-session scoping**: Currently, an operator must start a session with an estimated scope (e.g., \"all endpoints, user @alice\"), then watch it fill up / terminate, and re-analyze what went wrong. A dedicated War Room page should show a **Trace Map of live endpoint/level/source activity** (reusing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` component) *before* starting a session, letting the operator see the live shape of traffic and decide \"actually, I need to exclude `/sync` because it's too noisy\" — rather than starting the session blindly and hoping.\n27\t\n28\t3. **Convenience of \"exclusive\" filters**: The concept of \"exclude /sync\" arises from the observation that `/sync` (Matrix client long-poll) dominates any endpoint-scoped capture. This story adds an \"exclusive scope\" concept: instead of \"only capture scope X\", an operator can say \"capture everything except scope Y\" — a convenience syntax for \"no `/sync`\" (and potentially \"no `/health`\" or \"/live/longpoll\" — other noisy polling endpoints mentioned in the context).\n29\t\n30\t## Acceptance Criteria\n31\t\n32\t### War Room Session List View (`/admin/logs/war-room`)\n33\t\n34\t**AC1:** A new page `/admin/logs/war-room` (a LiveView) displays a **list of all War Room sessions** (active and historical), fetched from the `war_room_sessions` table and sorted with active sessions first, then by most recent start time.\n35\t\n36\t**AC2:** Each session row displays:\n37\t- **Session ID** (UUID, truncated or full — operator's choice)\n38\t- **Status badge** (🟢 Active, 🔴 Ended, with the end reason if known: \"budget_exhausted\", \"duration_exceeded\", \"manual_stop\")\n39\t- **Started by** (admin user_id who initiated the session)\n40\t- **Reason** (the free-text reason field from session creation)\n41\t- **Scope** (e.g., \"all\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", or a description of the scope)\n42\t- **Filter summary** (if a user_id/device_id filter was applied, show a compact label like \"user: @alice:localhost\")\n43\t- **Time range** (started_at → ended_at, or \"started X ago\" for active sessions)\n44\t- **Entry count** (entries_collected / max_entries, as a progress badge)\n45\t- **Duration** (elapsed time as a human-readable string: \"2 min 34 sec remaining\" for active, \"5 min 30 sec\" for ended)\n46\t\n47\t**AC3:** Clicking a session row navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}` to drill down into that session's details (see AC5 below).\n48\t\n49\t**AC4:** If no sessions exist (fresh deployment), the page shows a placeholder message: \"No War Room sessions yet. Start one from the monitoring page (`/admin/logs`) to begin diagnostic logging.\"\n50\t\n51\t### War Room Session Detail View (`/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}`)\n52\t\n53\t**AC5:** A session detail page shows:\n54\t- **Session metadata panel** (fixed at the top): the full session information (ID, started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries/max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected)\n55\t- **A read-only \"Logs captured by this session\" section** — the exact same request-stream list view from `/admin/logs` (reusing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s list and detail panel components), but FILTERED to show ONLY operational_logs entries where `war_room_id = {room_id}`, sorted by timestamp, with the trace-map disabled (no filter controls, since all entries already match the session's own scope/filter)\n56\t- **A copy-to-markdown button** for the full session metadata + entry list (for support hand-off, incident reports, etc.)\n57\t- **A breadcrumb or back link** to `/admin/logs/war-room` (list view)\n58\t\n59\t**AC6:** The detail view is accessible only if the session exists; if the room_id is not found, the page shows a 404 message and an offer to return to the list.\n60\t\n61\t### War Room Scoping Page (`/admin/logs/war-room` pre-session form)\n62\t\n63\t**AC7:** On the War Room list page, above the session list, there is a **\"Start a new War Room session\" form section** that lets an operator pre-scope a session before starting it. This form mirrors the existing embedded panel from war-room-mode but adds a **live Trace Map** to show the operator the live activity shape *before* they commit to a scope.\n64\t\n65\t**AC8:** The form includes:\n66\t- **A Trace Map of current live activity** (endpoints, levels, sources — identical UI to the monitoring page's trace map, but in `:configure` mode or a new `:scope` mode). This shows the operator \"here's what traffic looks like right now\" without committing them to collecting it yet. The map is live-updating (refreshing every 2s or on each new operational_logs entry via PubSub).\n67\t- **Reason** (text input, required)\n68\t- **Scope selector** (dropdown or toggle buttons):\n69\t  - `\"all\"` — capture all endpoints (default, shows AC7's global warning)\n70\t  - `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"` — capture only `/sync` (shows stronger AC7/AC8 warning; max_entries cap enforced)\n71\t  - `\"exclusive\"` mode with a nested **exclude-from** selector (new feature, see AC9–AC11 below)\n72\t- **Filter inputs** (optional):\n73\t  - `user_id` (text input, e.g., `@alice:localhost`)\n74\t  - `device_id` (text input, e.g., `GHTYAJSQZD`)\n75\t- **Budget inputs**:\n76\t  - `max_entries` (number input, default 100, range 10–10000)\n77\t  - `max_duration_s` (number input, default 300, range 10–3600)\n78\t- **A \"Start War Room\" button** (prominent, red/alert styling, `🚨 Start War Room`)\n79\t- **Contextual warnings** (AC7–AC8 from war-room-mode, re-displayed here)\n80\t\n81\t### \"Exclusive Scope\" Feature (Optional Narrowing, Design Decision Required)\n82\t\n83\t**AC9:** When scope is set to `\"exclusive\"`, a secondary **exclude-list selector** appears, allowing the operator to specify one or more endpoints to EXCLUDE from capture. The exclude list can be pre-populated with the **most common noisy endpoints**:\n84\t- `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"` — Matrix long-poll (the primary motivation)\n85\t- `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync/long\"` — if alias exists (verify in codebase)\n86\t- `\"/health\"` — health-check polling\n87\t- `\"/admin/live/longpoll\"` — admin push-notification polling\n88\t- Plus an \"other\" option to type a custom route\n89\t\n90\t**AC10:** If `exclusive` mode is selected with, say, `/sync` checked:\n91\t- The scope internally becomes `inclusive: \"all\", exclusive: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]` (new JSONB structure in `war_room_sessions.scope`)\n92\t- When `WarRoom.check/1` receives a log entry, it checks: (1) is the endpoint in the exclusive list? If yes, skip (do not tag, do not count). If no, tag and count normally.\n93\t- This is a convenience sugar over the existing logic — it avoids requiring an operator to say \"start a session for **all except sync**\" by laboriously testing which endpoints they want instead.\n94\t\n95\t**AC11 (Design Decision):** Should this story generalize \"exclusive sync\" to **\"configurable noisy-endpoint exclusion\"** for the Trace Map itself (not just War Room sessions)? In other words:\n96\t- Add a persistent **\"Hide noisy endpoints\" toggle** to `/admin/logs` (the monitoring page), hiding `/sync`, `/health`, `/live/longpoll` from the trace map and request stream by default\n97\t- Make the list of noisy endpoints operator-configurable (server_config key, e.g., `log_view_excluded_endpoints`)\n98\t- Apply this filtering to BOTH the monitoring page AND the War Room session form's Trace Map\n99\t\n100\t**Decision (for this story):** This story implements \"exclusive scope\" ONLY for War Room sessions (AC9–AC10). The broader monitoring-page filtering (AC11's generalization) is a separate, optional follow-up. However, if the implementation of AC8's \"scope Trace Map\" reveals that adding the exclusion logic is trivial (e.g., a single `filter_excluded/2` function called from both places), the developer SHOULD go ahead and implement AC11 as a bonus, documenting the scope decision in Dev Notes.\n101\t\n102\t### Nav Integration (Flat Nav, No New Sidebar)\n103\t\n104\t**AC12:** The existing flat nav in `Nebu.Admin.Layouts` (no nested/collapsible tree anywhere) does not need a new entry for the War Room list page. Instead:\n105\t- The existing `/admin/logs` monitoring page shows a link or button near the War Room embedded panel: \"📋 Full War Room Session List →\" or \"View all War Room sessions\" that navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n106\t- OR, a new `nav_item` is added to the sidebar for \"War Room\" that links to `/admin/logs/war-room`, with the understanding that `/admin/logs` itself (the monitoring page) can be reached via a back-link from the war-room page\n107\t\n108\t**Decision (for this story):** Add a direct nav_item \"War Room\" to the sidebar (next to \"Logs\" or grouped under \"Logs\"). If nested nav is ever built (separate epic: admin-sidebar-nesting), the structure can be refactored to put \"War Room\" as a child of \"Logs\". For now, keep it flat.\n109\t\n110\t### Accessibility\n111\t\n112\t**AC13:** The session list and detail views follow WCAG 2.1 AA standards:\n113\t- Session rows are native `<button>` or `<a>` elements (clickable)\n114\t- All status badges pair color with text (e.g., \"🟢 Active\", \"🔴 Ended (budget exhausted)\")\n115\t- The Trace Map in the form section (AC8) inherits the TraceMapBubble component's own a11y (AC13–AC14 from admin-logs-monitoring-view story)\n116\t- The page title and headings are semantic `<h1>`, `<h2>`\n117\t\n118\t## Acceptance Tests\n119\t\n120\t### Tests written FIRST (ATDD):\n121\t\n122\t1. **[AC1–AC2] War Room list page loads and displays sessions** — ExUnit integration test (Phoenix.LiveViewTest)\n123\t   - Given: 3 sessions in the war_room_sessions table (1 active, 2 ended)\n124\t   - When: LogsLive navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n125\t   - Then: a list of 3 rows is displayed, with the active session first\n126\t   - And: each row shows the expected fields (ID, status, reason, scope, time range, entry count, duration)\n127\t\n128\t2. **[AC2] Status badge reflects session state** — ExUnit integration test\n129\t   - Given: an active session (ended_at = NULL)\n130\t   - When: the list is rendered\n131\t   - Then: the status badge shows \"🟢 Active\"\n132\t   - Given: an ended session with ended_reason = \"budget_exhausted\"\n133\t   - When: the list is rendered\n134\t   - Then: the status badge shows \"🔴 Ended (budget exhausted)\"\n135\t\n136\t3. **[AC3] Clicking a row navigates to detail view** — ExUnit integration test\n137\t   - Given: a session with id=W in the list\n138\t   - When: the row is clicked\n139\t   - Then: the page navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room/{W}`\n140\t\n141\t4. **[AC4] Empty state message shown when no sessions exist** — ExUnit integration test\n142\t   - Given: an empty war_room_sessions table\n143\t   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room` is loaded\n144\t   - Then: the page displays \"No War Room sessions yet...\"\n145\t\n146\t5. **[AC5] Session detail view shows metadata and captured logs** — ExUnit integration test\n147\t   - Given: a session with id=W and 5 operational_logs entries tagged with war_room_id=W\n148\t   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room/{W}` is loaded\n149\t   - Then: the metadata panel displays the full session info\n150\t   - And: the logs section shows the 5 entries, sorted by timestamp\n151\t   - And: no entries with a different war_room_id are shown\n152\t\n153\t6. **[AC6] Detail view 404 for non-existent session** — ExUnit integration test\n154\t   - Given: an invalid session id\n155\t   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room/{invalid-id}` is loaded\n156\t   - Then: the page displays a 404 message\n157\t   - And: a link to return to `/admin/logs/war-room` is provided\n158\t\n159\t7. **[AC8] Pre-session form renders with all fields** — ExUnit integration test\n160\t   - Given: the `/admin/logs/war-room` list page\n161\t   - When: the page loads\n162\t   - Then: the \"Start a new War Room session\" form section is visible\n163\t   - And: it contains: reason input, scope selector, user_id/device_id inputs, max_entries/max_duration_s inputs, Start button\n164\t\n165\t8. **[AC8] Trace Map on the form is live-updating** — ExUnit integration test\n166\t   - Given: the form's Trace Map is displayed\n167\t   - When: a new operational_logs entry arrives via PubSub\n168\t   - Then: the Trace Map bubble counts update in real time (no page reload)\n169\t\n170\t9. **[AC9–AC10] Exclusive scope mode shows exclude-list selector** — ExUnit integration test\n171\t   - Given: the form with scope set to `\"exclusive\"`\n172\t   - When: the page re-renders\n173\t   - Then: an exclude-list selector appears with options like `/sync`, `/health`, `/live/longpoll`\n174\t   - And: the operator can check one or more to exclude them\n175\t\n176\t10. **[AC10] Starting a session with exclusive scope tags correctly** — ExUnit integration test\n177\t    - Given: a War Room is started with `scope: \"exclusive\"` and `exclusive: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]`\n178\t    - When: an operational_logs entry for `/sync` arrives\n179\t    - Then: it is NOT tagged with the war_room_id (excluded)\n180\t    - When: an entry for a different endpoint arrives\n181\t    - Then: it IS tagged (included)\n182\t\n183\t11. **[AC7–AC8] Warnings shown for broad/noisy scopes** — ExUnit integration test\n184\t    - Given: the form with scope set to `\"all\"`\n185\t    - When: the page renders\n186\t    - Then: a warning message appears: \"Warning: collecting all endpoints...\"\n187\t    - Given: scope set to `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"`\n188\t    - When: the page renders\n189\t    - Then: a stronger warning appears: \"⚠️ `/sync` is called continuously...\"\n190\t\n191\t12. **[AC12] Nav item for War Room page exists** — ExUnit integration test\n192\t    - Given: the admin sidebar\n193\t    - When: the page is loaded\n194\t    - Then: a \"War Room\" nav item is visible\n195\t    - And: it links to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n196\t\n197\t13. **[AC13] Accessibility: status badges are not color-only** — ExUnit test + manual audit\n198\t    - Given: a session row with a status badge\n199\t    - When: the HTML is inspected\n200\t    - Then: the badge contains both a colored emoji (🟢/🔴) and text (\"Active\"/\"Ended\"), not color alone\n201\t\n202\t14. **[AC5] Copy-to-markdown button exports session + logs** — ExUnit integration test\n203\t    - Given: a detail view with a session and 3 captured logs\n204\t    - When: the \"Copy to markdown\" button is clicked\n205\t    - Then: a text block is copied to clipboard containing session metadata + all logs in a readable format\n206\t    - And: the operator sees a toast/flash \"Copied to clipboard\"\n207\t\n208\t## Dev Notes\n209\t\n210\t### File Structure\n211\t\n212\t- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live.ex` — LiveView for `/admin/logs/war-room` (list + form)\n213\t  - Mount: load all sessions from `war_room_sessions` table, subscribe to `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub for live Trace Map updates\n214\t  - Render: session list (AC1–AC2), empty state (AC4), pre-session form with Trace Map (AC8), warnings (AC7–AC8)\n215\t  - Handle_info: receive operational_logs entries, update Trace Map bubble counts for the form\n216\t  - Handle_event: \"start_war_room\" event (calls `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1`), scope-mode toggle, exclusive-list updates\n217\t\n218\t- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_detail_live.ex` — LiveView for `/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}` (detail)\n219\t  - Mount: load session by id, fetch all operational_logs entries tagged with that war_room_id, subscribe to PubSub to receive new entries for this session in real time (AC5 — live-updating detail view)\n220\t  - Render: session metadata panel, logs section (reusing `detail_panel` component + request-stream subcomponents from LogsLive), copy-to-markdown button\n221\t  - Handle_info: receive operational_logs entries, filter to only this war_room_id, stream them into the logs view\n222\t  - Handle_event: \"copy_to_markdown\" event\n223\t\n224\t- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live/scope_selector.ex` — Phoenix.Component\n225\t  - Function component `scope_selector/1` — renders the scope selector with conditional exclusive-list UI\n226\t  - Accepts: `scope` value, `on_change` callback, `on_exclusive_change` for the exclude-list updates\n227\t\n228\t- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex`\n229\t  - Add two new routes under the `live_session :admin` block:\n230\t    - `live \"/logs/war-room\", WarRoomListLive, :index`\n231\t    - `live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\", WarRoomDetailLive, :show`\n232\t\n233\t- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`\n234\t  - Add a new nav_item for \"War Room\" (or \"War Room Sessions\"), linking to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n235\t\n236\t- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`\n237\t  - Add a link/button in the War Room embedded panel: \"View full session list →\" that navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n238\t  - (Alternatively: if AC12 decides to add a nav item instead, no change needed to LogsLive)\n239\t\n240\t- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/war_room.ex`\n241\t  - Update the scope-matching logic to support `exclusive` mode (AC9–AC10):\n242\t    - `scope` can now be a string or a map: `%{\"mode\" => \"inclusive\"} | %{\"mode\" => \"exclusive\", \"exclude\" => [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", ...]}`\n243\t    - `check/1` reads the mode and applies the exclude list logic\n244\t    - (Or, if simpler, `scope` remains a string and a new `exclusive_scope` JSONB column is added to `war_room_sessions` for the exclude list)\n245\t\n246\t- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex`\n247\t  - Update `start_session/1` to accept and validate `exclusive_scope` parameter (optional list of endpoints to exclude)\n248\t  - Pass it through to the session creation in the DB\n249\t\n250\t- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live_test.exs` — ExUnit tests for the list page\n251\t  - Tests 1–4, 7–9, 11–12, 14 above\n252\t\n253\t- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_detail_live_test.exs` — ExUnit tests for the detail page\n254\t  - Tests 5–6, 13 above (detail view specific)\n255\t\n256\t- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live/scope_selector_test.exs` — component tests\n257\t  - Test the scope_selector component rendering with different modes\n258\t\n259\t### Architecture & Implementation Hints\n260\t\n261\t1. **Session List Sorting & Filtering:**\n262\t   - Query: `SELECT * FROM war_room_sessions ORDER BY CASE WHEN ended_at IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, started_at DESC`\n263\t   - This sorts active sessions first, then by most recent start time\n264\t   - On load, fetch all sessions (or implement pagination if the table grows large)\n265\t\n266\t2. **Live Trace Map on Form (AC8):**\n267\t   - The form's Trace Map should be a **separate, stateful component** (not reusing the monitoring page's LogsLive trace map)\n268\t   - It subscribes to `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub in `WarRoomListLive.mount/3`\n269\t   - In `handle_info/2`, it receives entries and updates `@form_trace_bubble_counts` (a map)\n270\t   - The Trace Map renders with `mode: :filter` (using TraceMapBubble) but the bubbles are NOT clickable for filtering — they're read-only, just showing the live shape of traffic\n271\t   - (Alternative: `mode: :readonly` or `:view` — add to TraceMapBubble's mode allowlist, rendering bubbles that are not clickable at all)\n272\t\n273\t3. **Exclusive Scope Implementation:**\n274\t   - **Option A (recommended):** Add `exclusive_scope` as a new nullable JSONB column to `war_room_sessions`, separate from `scope`. `scope` remains a string (the inclusive scope: \"all\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", or NULL). `exclusive_scope` is an optional list `[\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/health\"]` when the operator uses the exclusive mode.\n275\t   - **Option B:** Change `scope` to a JSONB map `%{\"mode\" => \"inclusive\"|\"exclusive\", \"value\" => \"...\", \"exclude\" => [...]}` — requires more refactoring of existing war_room_mode code that reads `scope` as a string.\n276\t   - **Recommend Option A** — minimal changes to existing code, backward-compatible migration (`ADD COLUMN exclusive_scope JSONB`), and clearer semantics.\n277\t   - In `WarRoom.check/1`, match on `exclusive_scope` being non-nil and non-empty; if present, check if the entry's `route_template` is in the list before tagging.\n278\t\n279\t4. **Detail View Log Streaming:**\n280\t   - Load initial logs: `LogStore.get_logs(%{war_room_id: room_id, limit: 1000})` (or no limit, if reasonable)\n281\t   - In `handle_info/2`, receive operational_logs entries and filter to `entry.war_room_id == @room_id` before streaming\n282\t   - Reuse the request-stream list and detail panel components from `LogsLive` (they are already modular)\n283\t   - The detail view is read-only — no filter controls, just a chronological thread with the same layout as `/admin/logs`'s detail panel\n284\t\n285\t5. **Copy-to-Markdown (AC14):**\n286\t   - Build a Markdown string:\n287\t     ```\n288\t     # War Room Session {room_id}\n289\t     \n290\t     **Started by:** {started_by}\n291\t     **Reason:** {reason}\n292\t     **Scope:** {scope}\n293\t     **Filter:** {filter}\n294\t     **Time:** {started_at} → {ended_at}\n295\t     **Entries collected:** {entries_collected} / {max_entries}\n296\t     \n297\t     ## Captured Logs\n298\t     \n299\t     {for each entry}\n300\t     - `{timestamp}` [{level}] [{source}] {message}\n301\t     ```\n302\t   - Use a client-side library (Clipboard API via `navigator.clipboard.writeText()`) to copy to the user's clipboard\n303\t   - Show a toast/flash message \"Copied to clipboard\" via `JS.dispatch/2` or a `handle_event/3` round-trip\n304\t\n305\t6. **Trace Map Mode Decision (AC8's reuse of TraceMapBubble):**\n306\t   - The form's Trace Map shows live counts (identical to the monitoring page's) but is NOT filterable\n307\t   - Add a new TraceMapBubble mode `:view` (read-only, no phx-click) OR reuse `:filter` mode but pass a no-op `on_click` (e.g., `JS.noop()`)\n308\t   - Recommend `:readonly` as a new mode value in TraceMapBubble — clearly signals intent and allows the component to render a disabled/non-interactive button if needed\n309\t\n310\t7. **Exclusive Scope UI (AC9–AC10):**\n311\t   - The exclude-list selector can be a set of checkboxes, one per pre-defined noisy endpoint\n312\t   - Or, a multi-select dropdown with the same set\n313\t   - Store the selected list in the form's `phx-value-*` attributes or in a hidden input, passed to `start_war_room` event\n314\t   - In the LiveView, validate that the provided list contains only known, safe route_templates before inserting into the DB (defense-in-depth: do not trust the form's UI validation alone)\n315\t\n316\t### Testing Strategy\n317\t\n318\t1. **Unit tests (scope_selector component):** Render with different scope modes, verify the conditional UI.\n319\t2. **Integration tests (WarRoomListLive & WarRoomDetailLive):** Mount the views, verify session loading, live Trace Map updates, form submission, navigation, 404 handling.\n320\t3. **Exclusive scope logic:** Test `WarRoom.check/1` with exclusive_scope set, verify that excluded entries are not tagged.\n321\t4. **Accessibility audit:** Verify status badges, button states, nav items are all semantic.\n322\t5. **E2E test (optional):** A Playwright scenario that navigates to the War Room list, starts a session, verifies it appears in the list, clicks it, views the detail page, and copies the markdown.\n323\t\n324\t### Design Decision: Generalize to \"Noisy Endpoint Exclusion\" (AC11)\n325\t\n326\tIf AC11 is in scope (generalize exclusion to the monitoring page itself):\n327\t- Add a new `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.log_view_excluded_endpoints/0` helper that reads from `server_config` or a default list\n328\t- Call it in `LogsLive.mount/3` to seed `@excluded_endpoints`\n329\t- In the monitoring page's Trace Map rendering, filter out bubbles/entries for excluded endpoints\n330\t- In the War Room form, show the same list by default (read from config)\n331\t- Make it configurable via `/admin/config` (a new field in ConfigLive, added to `server_config`)\n332\t\n333\tIf AC11 is out of scope:\n334\t- Document in Dev Notes that this is a follow-up opportunity and note where the code would live\n335\t\n336\t## Tasks / Subtasks\n337\t\n338\tSuperseded by the \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\" section — the dev-story implemented the AMENDED design (one LiveView module, not three files) rather than the stale original plan below. Checked off against what was actually built:\n339\t\n340\t- [x] Create migration: `server/priv/migrations/000021_war_room_exclusive_scope.{up,down}.sql` — `ALTER TABLE war_room_sessions ADD COLUMN exclusive_scope JSONB` (nullable)\n341\t- [x] ~~Create `war_room_list_live.ex`~~ / ~~`war_room_detail_live.ex`~~ / ~~`scope_selector.ex`~~ — superseded by amendment 1: ONE module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` (`:index`/`:show` actions, no `handle_params/3`), mirroring `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own single-module shape. The exclusive-scope checkboxes are inline in the same module's form render, not a separate component.\n342\t- [x] Update `war_room_manager.ex` to accept and validate `exclusive_scope` parameter (shape validation, Dev Notes item 7)\n343\t- [x] Update `log_store/war_room.ex` to handle exclusive scope in `check/1`\n344\t- [x] Update `router.ex` — repoints the former `/logs/war-room` stub (amendment 1), adds `/logs/war-room/:room_id`\n345\t- [x] Update `layouts.ex` — sidebar recording dot + topbar recording pill (amendment 4); the \"War Room\" nav child already existed from `admin-sidebar-nesting`\n346\t- [x] Update `logs_live.ex` — embedded War Room panel REMOVED (amendment 2), not a link added; war-room status poll kept for the sidebar dot/topbar pill\n347\t- [x] Write acceptance tests FIRST (ATDD) — already staged before this dev-story cycle\n348\t- [x] Implement `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` (list mode + detail mode)\n349\t- [x] Verify all tests pass (unit suite 1557/1557; new-story integration suite verified per-file, see Dev Agent Record)\n350\t- [x] Manual accessibility audit — native `<a>`/`<button>` rows, paired emoji+text badges, `aria-live` copy toast; deferred full audit to Kassandra's security/UX review\n351\t- [ ] Security review (Kassandra) — pending, `security_review: required` per frontmatter\n352\t\n353\t## Cross-Story Dependencies & Notes\n354\t\n355\t### Depends On (must be ready before this story's dev):\n356\t- **war-room-mode** — provides the `war_room_sessions` table, `WarRoomManager` API, scope/filter matching logic, and the embedded panel UI this story extends\n357\t- **admin-logs-monitoring-view** — provides the request-stream list and detail-panel components that this story reuses on the detail view\n358\t- **operational-log-store** — provides the `operational_logs` table and the `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub topic for live updates\n359\t\n360\t### Coordination Notes:\n361\t- The `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` component is reused in the form (AC8). Confirm if a new mode (`:readonly` or `:view`) is needed, or if `:filter` with a no-op `on_click` is acceptable.\n362\t- The exclusive_scope logic in `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check/1` must be coordinated with the scope-matching design from war-room-mode. Verify that adding `exclusive_scope` does not conflict with the existing scope string matching.\n363\t- The session list/detail LiveViews reuse components from LogsLive (request stream, detail panel). Ensure these components are properly extracted as standalone, reusable modules if they are not already.\n364\t\n365\t## Design Decisions\n366\t\n367\t### AC11 Exclusion Scope (Noisy Endpoints)\n368\t**Decision:** This story implements exclusive scope ONLY for War Room sessions. The broader monitoring-page filtering is a follow-up. If the implementation reveals trivial generalization, it SHOULD be implemented as a bonus.\n369\t\n370\t### AC12 Nav Integration\n371\t**Decision:** Add a flat nav item \"War Room\" for `/admin/logs/war-room`. If nested nav is built later (separate epic), refactor then.\n372\t\n373\t### Exclusive Scope Schema (AC9–AC10)\n374\t**Decision:** Use Option A — add a separate `exclusive_scope JSONB` column to `war_room_sessions`, keeping `scope` as a string. This is backward-compatible and minimal-change.\n375\t\n376\t### Trace Map Mode for AC8\n377\t**Decision:** Add a new TraceMapBubble mode `:readonly` (or reuse `:filter` with no-op `on_click`). Document clearly that the form's Trace Map is read-only, not for filtering.\n378\t\n379\t## Dev Agent Record\n380\t\n381\t### Implementation Summary (dev-story cycle 0)\n382\t\n383\tImplemented per the story's \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\" section (authoritative over\n384\tthe stale original AC text): ONE LiveView module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`\n385\t(`:index`/`:show` actions, no `handle_params/3`), repointing the former\n386\t`/admin/logs/war-room` stub; the embedded War Room panel on `/admin/logs` was removed;\n387\t`TraceMapBubble` gained a `:readonly` mode reused for the level+source live-activity\n388\tpreview; `exclusive_scope` was added as a nullable JSONB column (migration 000021) and\n389\tthreaded through `WarRoomManager.start_session/1` (shape-validated, not a closed\n390\tallowlist) and `WarRoom.check/1`; the sidebar recording dot / topbar recording pill were\n391\tadded to `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` via a new shared `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator`\n392\thelper, wired into every remaining admin LiveView (`DashboardLive`, `AuditLogLive`,\n393\t`LogsLive`, `LogsLive.TraceMap`, `LogsLive.WarRoom` — the full \"anywhere in the admin UI\"\n394\tsurface after admin-http-refactor's controller conversions); copy-to-markdown reuses the\n395\texisting `nebu:copy-thread` CSP-safe client event.\n396\t\n397\tAn \"AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one active session\" banner (reusing the retired\n398\tembedded panel's own `war-room-panel`/`stop-war-room-button` testids) was added to the\n399\tdedicated page's list view, replacing the start form while a session is active — this\n400\twas NOT in the original story text but was required to keep the pre-existing\n401\t`war-room.feature`/`logs-trace-map.feature` E2E scenarios' reused step definitions\n402\t(`the operator starts/stops a War Room`) working against the new page.\n403\t\n404\t### Bugs found and fixed during E2E verification (outside the story's own file list, but\n405\trequired to make the ATDD/E2E suite genuinely green)\n406\t\n407\t- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: `runSqlReturningId` didn't strip psql's trailing command-tag line\n408\t  (\"INSERT 0 1\") from its `-t -A` output, corrupting every ID it returned — fixed to take\n409\t  only the first line.\n410\t- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: `clearWarRoomSessions` didn't delete the referencing\n411\t  `operational_logs` rows first, so it failed with an FK-constraint violation once any\n412\t  scenario had attached captured entries to a session — fixed to delete children first.\n413\t- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: removed a duplicate `endActiveWarRoomSessions` (already existed\n414\t  from `trace-map-node-controls`) that this story's own staged diff re-added, which broke\n415\t  `bddgen`'s TypeScript compile entirely (`SyntaxError: Identifier ... has already been\n416\t  declared`).\n417\t- `e2e/features/admin/war-room.feature`: retired (embedded panel scenarios), mirroring\n418\t  the ExUnit-side `war_room_panel_retirement_test.exs`.\n419\t- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`: its own `@ac6-war-room-banner` scenario\n420\t  started a session via the now-retired embedded panel on `/admin/logs` — repointed to\n421\t  start it via the new `/admin/logs/war-room` page instead (same reused step\n422\t  definitions).\n423\t\n424\t### Round 2 fixes (coordinator-reported, 2026-07-08 — after a clean `war_room_sessions`\n425\ttruncate isolated 4 real bugs from the earlier round's environmental noise)\n426\t\n427\t1. **Cross-scenario pollution WITHIN one suite run.** Every scenario in\n428\t   `war-room-dedicated-page.feature` seeds a session and nothing cleaned up between\n429\t   scenarios (only the single `@ac4` scenario's own \"no sessions exist\" step called\n430\t   `clearWarRoomSessions`). Fixed by tagging every scenario `@war-room-dedicated-page` and\n431\t   registering `After({ tags: '@war-room-dedicated-page' }, () => clearWarRoomSessions())`\n432\t   in the steps file — mirrors `logs-trace-map.steps.ts`'s own `@node-controls`\n433\t   tag-scoped-hook pattern exactly. Also broadened `clearWarRoomSessions`'s WHERE clause\n434\t   (`started_by LIKE '@e2e-wr-%' OR reason LIKE 'e2e %'`) since the AC9/AC10 scenario\n435\t   starts its session through the REAL UI, so its `started_by` is the logged-in e2e\n436\t   admin's real id, never `@e2e-wr-%` — only its literal `reason` (\"e2e exclusive scope\n437\t   test\") is a usable cleanup marker for that row.\n438\t2. **Exclusive-scope options never appeared.** Root cause: NOT a template/testid bug —\n439\t   `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`'s scope `<select>` correctly reveals\n440\t   `war-room-exclusive-options` via a `phx-change` round trip (proven by the ExUnit\n441\t   `render_change` test, which passed all along). The shared `war-room.steps.ts`'s\n442\t   \"the operator selects {string} as the War Room scope\" step was missing the\n443\t   `[data-phx-main].phx-connected` wait its own sibling \"starts a War Room...\" step already\n444\t   has — on the throttled-longpoll transport, `selectOption()` fired before the LiveSocket\n445\t   connected, so the native `change` event landed on a dead, unconnected static DOM and\n446\t   the server round trip never happened. Fixed by adding the same wait.\n447\t3. **Copy-to-markdown toast never appeared.** Same root cause as #2: \"the operator clicks\n448\t   the copy-to-markdown button\" (`war-room-dedicated-page.steps.ts`) had no\n449\t   `phx-connected` wait. The button's `phx-click` is\n450\t   `JS.dispatch(\"nebu:copy-thread\") |> JS.push(\"copy_to_markdown\")` — the `dispatch` half\n451\t   is pure client-side (fires even pre-connection, which is why the clipboard write could\n452\t   still silently succeed) but the `push` that actually sets `@copy_toast` server-side\n453\t   needs a live socket. Fixed by adding the wait.\n454\t4. **Keyboard-operability scenario: `select[name=\"scope\"]` never found.** Same root cause\n455\t   as #2 (this scenario reuses the same buggy shared step) — fixing #2 fixed this too.\n456\t\n457\t### Verification (final, after round 2)\n458\t\n459\t- `mix test --warnings-as-errors` (no `NEBU_DB_URL`, mirrors `make test-unit-elixir`):\n460\t  **1557/1557 passed, 0 failures, exit code 0** (also fixed an unrelated pre-existing\n461\t  `--warnings-as-errors`-fatal compile warning in `war_room_test.exs`'s\n462\t  `insert_operational_log/2` helper — an unused default argument value — that was silently\n463\t  aborting the run even though every individual test passed).\n464\t- `mix test --include integration` targeted at every new/touched test file: passes\n465\t  cleanly in isolation (91-98 tests depending on grouping).\n466\t- E2E (`--project=admin-ui --grep \"War Room\"`): **10/10 passed** against a genuinely clean\n467\t  `war_room_sessions` table (confirmed empty after the run via the new After-hook).\n468\t- E2E regression sweep (`--grep \"Trace Map|Node Controls|sidebar\"`, 23 scenarios): all\n469\t  green (one intermittent, unrelated node-overlap click flake in\n470\t  `logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature` — pure geometry/traffic-volume timing, not\n471\t  touched by this story — passed cleanly on retry).\n472\t\n473\t## Design Update (2026-07-07 — Trace Map Leitstand)\n474\t\n475\tPhil approved an interactive design dummy covering this story's page shell. New ACs to\n476\tincorporate (see `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md` §6 + `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`):\n477\t- While a session is active: pulsing red recording dot on the \"War Room\" sidebar item\n478\t  and a topbar recording pill (\"War Room recording · mm:ss\", user + elapsed time).\n479\t- The red across-the-view banner on the Trace Map page is scoped to the trace-map story,\n480\t  driven by the same session status.\n481\t\n482\t## Story Amendments (2026-07-08, coordinator/design owner — realignment after the shipped Leitstand redesign)\n483\t\n484\tThis story predates `admin-sidebar-nesting`, `trace-map-leitstand`, and `trace-map-node-controls`\n485\t(all committed since). Several ACs/Dev Notes below are stale and are corrected here; the\n486\tnumbered ACs above stay the source of truth for everything NOT touched by this section.\n487\t\n488\t1. **AC12 / nav integration — MOOT, already shipped differently.** The sidebar's \"Logs\"\n489\t   group already has a \"War Room\" child (navkey `logs-war-room`, `admin-sidebar-nesting`).\n490\t   It currently points at a STUB route (`live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive, :index` in\n491\t   `router.ex` — literally the Live Stream page, mislabeled). This story's job is to\n492\t   **replace the stub**: `live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive.WarRoom, :index` (new LiveView,\n493\t   naming consistent with the sibling `LogsLive.TraceMap` module) with\n494\t   `@active_nav = \"logs-war-room\"`, plus `live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\", LogsLive.WarRoom, :show`\n495\t   for the detail view (same module, two `handle_params` shaped actions — mirror however\n496\t   `LogsLive.TraceMap` structures its own single-LiveView, multi-concern pattern; do NOT\n497\t   invent a second top-level nav entry).\n498\t\n499\t2. **Embedded panel retirement.** `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomPanel.war_room_panel/1` is\n500\t   STILL embedded on `/admin/logs` (Live Stream page, `logs_live.ex:1007`) — this story's\n501\t   own premise (\"dedicated page ... rather than only an embedded panel\") means it must be\n502\t   REMOVED from Live Stream once the dedicated page ships (mirrors exactly how\n503\t   `trace-map-leitstand` retired the embedded Trace Map from that same page). The\n504\t   \"start a new session\" form (AC7/AC8) moves to the new page entirely. `WarRoomPanel`'s\n505\t   status-badge/warning sub-components may be reused by the new page if convenient; the\n506\t   component itself no longer renders on Live Stream.\n507\t\n508\t3. **AC8's Trace Map reuse — `TraceMapBubble`, NOT the new radial Leitstand map.**\n509\t   `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` (level/source dimensions) is unchanged and still\n510\t   live on `/admin/logs`; only the ENDPOINT dimension moved to the new radial\n511\t   `LogsLive.TraceMap` page. AC8's \"live activity shape\" preview on the scoping form\n512\t   should reuse `TraceMapBubble` for **level + source** only (a lightweight, existing,\n513\t   already-accessible component) — do NOT build a second radial-map instance for this\n514\t   small preview widget; that is out of proportion to the need (the exclusive-scope\n515\t   checkboxes, AC9–AC11, already solve the actual \"sync is noisy\" motivation directly).\n516\t   Add the `:readonly` mode to `TraceMapBubble` per the story's own Trace Map Mode\n517\t   Decision (unchanged, still correct).\n518\t\n519\t4. **War-room visibility in the sidebar/topbar (closes the gap left after\n520\t   `trace-map-leitstand` shipped only the in-page banner):** while a session is active,\n521\t   add (a) a pulsing recording dot on the sidebar's \"War Room\" nav item\n522\t   (`server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex` `nav_item`, dot shown when\n523\t   `@war_room_active` — a new assign threaded through `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`, sourced\n524\t   from `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0`, polled the same way `LogsLive`/`LogsLive.TraceMap`\n525\t   already do) and (b) a recording pill in the topbar (\"War Room recording · mm:ss\") per\n526\t   the approved dummy (`trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` topbar `.pill--warroom`). This was\n527\t   scoped to `war-room-dedicated-page` by the `trace-map-leitstand` UX gate's A2/coordination\n528\t   note and by this story's own original 2026-07-07 Design Update block below.\n529\t   CSP: countdown/dot ticking via the existing `app.js` polling pattern, no inline JS.\n530\t\n531\t5. **Exclusive-scope endpoint list — reuse the canonical constant.** AC9's pre-populated\n532\t   noisy-endpoint list overlaps with `trace-map-node-controls`'s\n533\t   `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0` (`/_matrix/client/v3/sync`,\n534\t   `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`). REUSE that public function as the default exclude-list\n535\t   source instead of hardcoding a parallel list (verify each AC9-named endpoint actually\n536\t   exists as a real route first — `/_matrix/client/v3/sync/long` and\n537\t   `/admin/live/longpoll` were speculative in the original AC9 text; only include real,\n538\t   observed route templates plus a free-text \"other\" input, exactly as AC9 already allows).\n539\t\n540\t6. **Copy-to-markdown (AC14) CSP compliance.** `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` must be\n541\t   wired through the existing `app.js` hook pattern (a small dedicated hook or an\n542\t   extension of an existing one) — no inline `onclick`/`phx-click` JS. Toast/flash via a\n543\t   `handle_event` round-trip is fine and matches the rest of the admin UI.\n544\t\n545\t7. **Security review stays REQUIRED** (already set in frontmatter) — this story writes\n546\t   admin-supplied exclusive-scope lists into session records and exposes historical log\n547\t   content on the detail page; Kassandra should pay particular attention to (a) the\n548\t   defense-in-depth validation AC9/Dev-Notes-item-7 already calls for (don't trust\n549\t   client-supplied route strings unchecked) and (b) whether the detail view's log display\n550\t   needs the same No-Sensitive-Data treatment as the Leitstand stream panel.\n551\t\n552\t## File List\n553\t\n554\tNew:\n555\t- `server/priv/migrations/000021_war_room_exclusive_scope.up.sql`\n556\t- `server/priv/migrations/000021_war_room_exclusive_scope.down.sql`\n557\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex`\n558\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_indicator.ex`\n559\t\n560\tModified:\n561\t- `server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/war_room.ex`\n562\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex`\n563\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble.ex`\n564\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex`\n565\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`\n566\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`\n567\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex`\n568\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/dashboard_live.ex`\n569\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/audit_log_live.ex`\n570\t- `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`\n571\t- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live_test.exs` (removed the two retired-panel describe blocks)\n572\t- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room_test.exs` (UUID canonicalization fix in the\n573\t  shared test helpers)\n574\t- `server/test/nebu/web/log_store/war_room_test.exs` (already staged; unmodified beyond\n575\t  the ATDD addition)\n576\t- `server/test/nebu/release_test.exs` (migration count bump to 21)\n577\t- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts` (bug fixes: `runSqlReturningId` multi-line output,\n578\t  `clearWarRoomSessions` FK-order + broadened WHERE clause, duplicate\n579\t  `endActiveWarRoomSessions` removed)\n580\t- `e2e/features/admin/war-room.feature` (retired)\n581\t- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` (`@ac6-war-room-banner` repointed to the new page)\n582\t- `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature` (every scenario tagged\n583\t  `@war-room-dedicated-page` for the new After-hook)\n584\t- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.steps.ts` (After-hook registration;\n585\t  `phx-connected` wait added to the \"checks... excluded endpoint\" and \"clicks the\n586\t  copy-to-markdown button\" steps)\n587\t- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/war-room.steps.ts` (`phx-connected` wait added to the\n588\t  standalone \"selects... War Room scope\" step)\n589\t- `docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md` (this file)\n590\t\n591\t## Change Log\n592\t\n593\t- 2026-07-08 (dev-story cycle 0): Implemented the amended design (single `LogsLive.WarRoom`\n594\t  module, embedded-panel retirement, `:readonly` TraceMapBubble mode, `exclusive_scope`\n595\t  column + validation, sidebar dot/topbar pill via `WarRoomIndicator`, CSP-safe\n596\t  copy-to-markdown). Fixed 3 pre-existing bugs in `e2e/fixtures/db.ts` and retired/repointed\n597\t  2 E2E feature files' War Room dependencies discovered during live-stack verification.\n598\t- 2026-07-08 (round 2, coordinator-reported): fixed cross-scenario `war_room_sessions`\n599\t  pollution (new tag-scoped After-hook), a missing `phx-connected` wait in 3 shared E2E\n600\t  step definitions (exclusive-scope select, copy-to-markdown click — the actual root cause\n601\t  behind 3 of the 4 reported failures), and an unrelated pre-existing\n602\t  `--warnings-as-errors`-fatal compile warning in a test helper. E2E War Room suite now\n603\t  10/10; `make test-unit-elixir` 1557/1557 exit 0.\n604\t"}]},"uuid":"98859a06-a4d1-4cb3-b1fb-f82d657b1e86","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:19:29.881Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md","content":"---\nid: war-room-dedicated-page\ntitle: \"War Room as a dedicated sub-page with session list, scoping, and historical view\"\nepic: enhanced-logging\nstatus: ready-for-dev\nsecurity_review: required\nmatrix: false\nui: true\ncreated: 2026-07-06\n---\n\n# War Room as a Dedicated Sub-Page\n\n## Story\n\n**As** a Nebu platform operator,\n**I want** a dedicated `/admin/logs/war-room` page that lists active and historical War Room sessions with full session details, and the ability to drill down into a session to view all logs captured by that session, rather than accessing War Room only as an embedded panel on `/admin/logs`,\n**so that** I can discover, review, and investigate diagnostic sessions without searching through a crowded monitoring page, pre-scope a session before starting it to avoid capturing unwanted high-frequency traffic, and maintain a permanent audit trail of which sessions captured what and when.\n\n## Background\n\nThe `war-room-mode` story (delivered) implemented War Room as an embedded inline panel on `/admin/logs`: a form with `max_entries`/`max_duration_s` inputs and a \"🚨 Start War Room\" button, backed by the `war_room_sessions` table and `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` API. The operator who requested this feature reviewed the shipped version in production and found two usability gaps:\n\n1. **Discoverability and session review**: The panel is cramped on an already-full monitoring page. Operators need a proper list view of all sessions (active + historical ended) to review which sessions exist, who started them, what scope/filters were applied, and when. This also creates an audit trail that survives the next monitoring-page reload or admin logout.\n\n2. **Pre-session scoping**: Currently, an operator must start a session with an estimated scope (e.g., \"all endpoints, user @alice\"), then watch it fill up / terminate, and re-analyze what went wrong. A dedicated War Room page should show a **Trace Map of live endpoint/level/source activity** (reusing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` component) *before* starting a session, letting the operator see the live shape of traffic and decide \"actually, I need to exclude `/sync` because it's too noisy\" — rather than starting the session blindly and hoping.\n\n3. **Convenience of \"exclusive\" filters**: The concept of \"exclude /sync\" arises from the observation that `/sync` (Matrix client long-poll) dominates any endpoint-scoped capture. This story adds an \"exclusive scope\" concept: instead of \"only capture scope X\", an operator can say \"capture everything except scope Y\" — a convenience syntax for \"no `/sync`\" (and potentially \"no `/health`\" or \"/live/longpoll\" — other noisy polling endpoints mentioned in the context).\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n### War Room Session List View (`/admin/logs/war-room`)\n\n**AC1:** A new page `/admin/logs/war-room` (a LiveView) displays a **list of all War Room sessions** (active and historical), fetched from the `war_room_sessions` table and sorted with active sessions first, then by most recent start time.\n\n**AC2:** Each session row displays:\n- **Session ID** (UUID, truncated or full — operator's choice)\n- **Status badge** (🟢 Active, 🔴 Ended, with the end reason if known: \"budget_exhausted\", \"duration_exceeded\", \"manual_stop\")\n- **Started by** (admin user_id who initiated the session)\n- **Reason** (the free-text reason field from session creation)\n- **Scope** (e.g., \"all\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", or a description of the scope)\n- **Filter summary** (if a user_id/device_id filter was applied, show a compact label like \"user: @alice:localhost\")\n- **Time range** (started_at → ended_at, or \"started X ago\" for active sessions)\n- **Entry count** (entries_collected / max_entries, as a progress badge)\n- **Duration** (elapsed time as a human-readable string: \"2 min 34 sec remaining\" for active, \"5 min 30 sec\" for ended)\n\n**AC3:** Clicking a session row navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}` to drill down into that session's details (see AC5 below).\n\n**AC4:** If no sessions exist (fresh deployment), the page shows a placeholder message: \"No War Room sessions yet. Start one from the monitoring page (`/admin/logs`) to begin diagnostic logging.\"\n\n### War Room Session Detail View (`/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}`)\n\n**AC5:** A session detail page shows:\n- **Session metadata panel** (fixed at the top): the full session information (ID, started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries/max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected)\n- **A read-only \"Logs captured by this session\" section** — the exact same request-stream list view from `/admin/logs` (reusing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s list and detail panel components), but FILTERED to show ONLY operational_logs entries where `war_room_id = {room_id}`, sorted by timestamp, with the trace-map disabled (no filter controls, since all entries already match the session's own scope/filter)\n- **A copy-to-markdown button** for the full session metadata + entry list (for support hand-off, incident reports, etc.)\n- **A breadcrumb or back link** to `/admin/logs/war-room` (list view)\n\n**AC6:** The detail view is accessible only if the session exists; if the room_id is not found, the page shows a 404 message and an offer to return to the list.\n\n### War Room Scoping Page (`/admin/logs/war-room` pre-session form)\n\n**AC7:** On the War Room list page, above the session list, there is a **\"Start a new War Room session\" form section** that lets an operator pre-scope a session before starting it. This form mirrors the existing embedded panel from war-room-mode but adds a **live Trace Map** to show the operator the live activity shape *before* they commit to a scope.\n\n**AC8:** The form includes:\n- **A Trace Map of current live activity** (endpoints, levels, sources — identical UI to the monitoring page's trace map, but in `:configure` mode or a new `:scope` mode). This shows the operator \"here's what traffic looks like right now\" without committing them to collecting it yet. The map is live-updating (refreshing every 2s or on each new operational_logs entry via PubSub).\n- **Reason** (text input, required)\n- **Scope selector** (dropdown or toggle buttons):\n  - `\"all\"` — capture all endpoints (default, shows AC7's global warning)\n  - `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"` — capture only `/sync` (shows stronger AC7/AC8 warning; max_entries cap enforced)\n  - `\"exclusive\"` mode with a nested **exclude-from** selector (new feature, see AC9–AC11 below)\n- **Filter inputs** (optional):\n  - `user_id` (text input, e.g., `@alice:localhost`)\n  - `device_id` (text input, e.g., `GHTYAJSQZD`)\n- **Budget inputs**:\n  - `max_entries` (number input, default 100, range 10–10000)\n  - `max_duration_s` (number input, default 300, range 10–3600)\n- **A \"Start War Room\" button** (prominent, red/alert styling, `🚨 Start War Room`)\n- **Contextual warnings** (AC7–AC8 from war-room-mode, re-displayed here)\n\n### \"Exclusive Scope\" Feature (Optional Narrowing, Design Decision Required)\n\n**AC9:** When scope is set to `\"exclusive\"`, a secondary **exclude-list selector** appears, allowing the operator to specify one or more endpoints to EXCLUDE from capture. The exclude list can be pre-populated with the **most common noisy endpoints**:\n- `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"` — Matrix long-poll (the primary motivation)\n- `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync/long\"` — if alias exists (verify in codebase)\n- `\"/health\"` — health-check polling\n- `\"/admin/live/longpoll\"` — admin push-notification polling\n- Plus an \"other\" option to type a custom route\n\n**AC10:** If `exclusive` mode is selected with, say, `/sync` checked:\n- The scope internally becomes `inclusive: \"all\", exclusive: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]` (new JSONB structure in `war_room_sessions.scope`)\n- When `WarRoom.check/1` receives a log entry, it checks: (1) is the endpoint in the exclusive list? If yes, skip (do not tag, do not count). If no, tag and count normally.\n- This is a convenience sugar over the existing logic — it avoids requiring an operator to say \"start a session for **all except sync**\" by laboriously testing which endpoints they want instead.\n\n**AC11 (Design Decision):** Should this story generalize \"exclusive sync\" to **\"configurable noisy-endpoint exclusion\"** for the Trace Map itself (not just War Room sessions)? In other words:\n- Add a persistent **\"Hide noisy endpoints\" toggle** to `/admin/logs` (the monitoring page), hiding `/sync`, `/health`, `/live/longpoll` from the trace map and request stream by default\n- Make the list of noisy endpoints operator-configurable (server_config key, e.g., `log_view_excluded_endpoints`)\n- Apply this filtering to BOTH the monitoring page AND the War Room session form's Trace Map\n\n**Decision (for this story):** This story implements \"exclusive scope\" ONLY for War Room sessions (AC9–AC10). The broader monitoring-page filtering (AC11's generalization) is a separate, optional follow-up. However, if the implementation of AC8's \"scope Trace Map\" reveals that adding the exclusion logic is trivial (e.g., a single `filter_excluded/2` function called from both places), the developer SHOULD go ahead and implement AC11 as a bonus, documenting the scope decision in Dev Notes.\n\n### Nav Integration (Flat Nav, No New Sidebar)\n\n**AC12:** The existing flat nav in `Nebu.Admin.Layouts` (no nested/collapsible tree anywhere) does not need a new entry for the War Room list page. Instead:\n- The existing `/admin/logs` monitoring page shows a link or button near the War Room embedded panel: \"📋 Full War Room Session List →\" or \"View all War Room sessions\" that navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n- OR, a new `nav_item` is added to the sidebar for \"War Room\" that links to `/admin/logs/war-room`, with the understanding that `/admin/logs` itself (the monitoring page) can be reached via a back-link from the war-room page\n\n**Decision (for this story):** Add a direct nav_item \"War Room\" to the sidebar (next to \"Logs\" or grouped under \"Logs\"). If nested nav is ever built (separate epic: admin-sidebar-nesting), the structure can be refactored to put \"War Room\" as a child of \"Logs\". For now, keep it flat.\n\n### Accessibility\n\n**AC13:** The session list and detail views follow WCAG 2.1 AA standards:\n- Session rows are native `<button>` or `<a>` elements (clickable)\n- All status badges pair color with text (e.g., \"🟢 Active\", \"🔴 Ended (budget exhausted)\")\n- The Trace Map in the form section (AC8) inherits the TraceMapBubble component's own a11y (AC13–AC14 from admin-logs-monitoring-view story)\n- The page title and headings are semantic `<h1>`, `<h2>`\n\n## Acceptance Tests\n\n### Tests written FIRST (ATDD):\n\n1. **[AC1–AC2] War Room list page loads and displays sessions** — ExUnit integration test (Phoenix.LiveViewTest)\n   - Given: 3 sessions in the war_room_sessions table (1 active, 2 ended)\n   - When: LogsLive navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n   - Then: a list of 3 rows is displayed, with the active session first\n   - And: each row shows the expected fields (ID, status, reason, scope, time range, entry count, duration)\n\n2. **[AC2] Status badge reflects session state** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: an active session (ended_at = NULL)\n   - When: the list is rendered\n   - Then: the status badge shows \"🟢 Active\"\n   - Given: an ended session with ended_reason = \"budget_exhausted\"\n   - When: the list is rendered\n   - Then: the status badge shows \"🔴 Ended (budget exhausted)\"\n\n3. **[AC3] Clicking a row navigates to detail view** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: a session with id=W in the list\n   - When: the row is clicked\n   - Then: the page navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room/{W}`\n\n4. **[AC4] Empty state message shown when no sessions exist** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: an empty war_room_sessions table\n   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room` is loaded\n   - Then: the page displays \"No War Room sessions yet...\"\n\n5. **[AC5] Session detail view shows metadata and captured logs** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: a session with id=W and 5 operational_logs entries tagged with war_room_id=W\n   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room/{W}` is loaded\n   - Then: the metadata panel displays the full session info\n   - And: the logs section shows the 5 entries, sorted by timestamp\n   - And: no entries with a different war_room_id are shown\n\n6. **[AC6] Detail view 404 for non-existent session** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: an invalid session id\n   - When: `/admin/logs/war-room/{invalid-id}` is loaded\n   - Then: the page displays a 404 message\n   - And: a link to return to `/admin/logs/war-room` is provided\n\n7. **[AC8] Pre-session form renders with all fields** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: the `/admin/logs/war-room` list page\n   - When: the page loads\n   - Then: the \"Start a new War Room session\" form section is visible\n   - And: it contains: reason input, scope selector, user_id/device_id inputs, max_entries/max_duration_s inputs, Start button\n\n8. **[AC8] Trace Map on the form is live-updating** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: the form's Trace Map is displayed\n   - When: a new operational_logs entry arrives via PubSub\n   - Then: the Trace Map bubble counts update in real time (no page reload)\n\n9. **[AC9–AC10] Exclusive scope mode shows exclude-list selector** — ExUnit integration test\n   - Given: the form with scope set to `\"exclusive\"`\n   - When: the page re-renders\n   - Then: an exclude-list selector appears with options like `/sync`, `/health`, `/live/longpoll`\n   - And: the operator can check one or more to exclude them\n\n10. **[AC10] Starting a session with exclusive scope tags correctly** — ExUnit integration test\n    - Given: a War Room is started with `scope: \"exclusive\"` and `exclusive: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]`\n    - When: an operational_logs entry for `/sync` arrives\n    - Then: it is NOT tagged with the war_room_id (excluded)\n    - When: an entry for a different endpoint arrives\n    - Then: it IS tagged (included)\n\n11. **[AC7–AC8] Warnings shown for broad/noisy scopes** — ExUnit integration test\n    - Given: the form with scope set to `\"all\"`\n    - When: the page renders\n    - Then: a warning message appears: \"Warning: collecting all endpoints...\"\n    - Given: scope set to `\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"`\n    - When: the page renders\n    - Then: a stronger warning appears: \"⚠️ `/sync` is called continuously...\"\n\n12. **[AC12] Nav item for War Room page exists** — ExUnit integration test\n    - Given: the admin sidebar\n    - When: the page is loaded\n    - Then: a \"War Room\" nav item is visible\n    - And: it links to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n\n13. **[AC13] Accessibility: status badges are not color-only** — ExUnit test + manual audit\n    - Given: a session row with a status badge\n    - When: the HTML is inspected\n    - Then: the badge contains both a colored emoji (🟢/🔴) and text (\"Active\"/\"Ended\"), not color alone\n\n14. **[AC5] Copy-to-markdown button exports session + logs** — ExUnit integration test\n    - Given: a detail view with a session and 3 captured logs\n    - When: the \"Copy to markdown\" button is clicked\n    - Then: a text block is copied to clipboard containing session metadata + all logs in a readable format\n    - And: the operator sees a toast/flash \"Copied to clipboard\"\n\n## Dev Notes\n\n### File Structure\n\n- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live.ex` — LiveView for `/admin/logs/war-room` (list + form)\n  - Mount: load all sessions from `war_room_sessions` table, subscribe to `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub for live Trace Map updates\n  - Render: session list (AC1–AC2), empty state (AC4), pre-session form with Trace Map (AC8), warnings (AC7–AC8)\n  - Handle_info: receive operational_logs entries, update Trace Map bubble counts for the form\n  - Handle_event: \"start_war_room\" event (calls `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1`), scope-mode toggle, exclusive-list updates\n\n- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_detail_live.ex` — LiveView for `/admin/logs/war-room/{room_id}` (detail)\n  - Mount: load session by id, fetch all operational_logs entries tagged with that war_room_id, subscribe to PubSub to receive new entries for this session in real time (AC5 — live-updating detail view)\n  - Render: session metadata panel, logs section (reusing `detail_panel` component + request-stream subcomponents from LogsLive), copy-to-markdown button\n  - Handle_info: receive operational_logs entries, filter to only this war_room_id, stream them into the logs view\n  - Handle_event: \"copy_to_markdown\" event\n\n- **New:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live/scope_selector.ex` — Phoenix.Component\n  - Function component `scope_selector/1` — renders the scope selector with conditional exclusive-list UI\n  - Accepts: `scope` value, `on_change` callback, `on_exclusive_change` for the exclude-list updates\n\n- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex`\n  - Add two new routes under the `live_session :admin` block:\n    - `live \"/logs/war-room\", WarRoomListLive, :index`\n    - `live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\", WarRoomDetailLive, :show`\n\n- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`\n  - Add a new nav_item for \"War Room\" (or \"War Room Sessions\"), linking to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n\n- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`\n  - Add a link/button in the War Room embedded panel: \"View full session list →\" that navigates to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n  - (Alternatively: if AC12 decides to add a nav item instead, no change needed to LogsLive)\n\n- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/war_room.ex`\n  - Update the scope-matching logic to support `exclusive` mode (AC9–AC10):\n    - `scope` can now be a string or a map: `%{\"mode\" => \"inclusive\"} | %{\"mode\" => \"exclusive\", \"exclude\" => [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", ...]}`\n    - `check/1` reads the mode and applies the exclude list logic\n    - (Or, if simpler, `scope` remains a string and a new `exclusive_scope` JSONB column is added to `war_room_sessions` for the exclude list)\n\n- **Modified:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex`\n  - Update `start_session/1` to accept and validate `exclusive_scope` parameter (optional list of endpoints to exclude)\n  - Pass it through to the session creation in the DB\n\n- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live_test.exs` — ExUnit tests for the list page\n  - Tests 1–4, 7–9, 11–12, 14 above\n\n- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_detail_live_test.exs` — ExUnit tests for the detail page\n  - Tests 5–6, 13 above (detail view specific)\n\n- **New:** `server/test/nebu/admin/war_room_list_live/scope_selector_test.exs` — component tests\n  - Test the scope_selector component rendering with different modes\n\n### Architecture & Implementation Hints\n\n1. **Session List Sorting & Filtering:**\n   - Query: `SELECT * FROM war_room_sessions ORDER BY CASE WHEN ended_at IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, started_at DESC`\n   - This sorts active sessions first, then by most recent start time\n   - On load, fetch all sessions (or implement pagination if the table grows large)\n\n2. **Live Trace Map on Form (AC8):**\n   - The form's Trace Map should be a **separate, stateful component** (not reusing the monitoring page's LogsLive trace map)\n   - It subscribes to `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub in `WarRoomListLive.mount/3`\n   - In `handle_info/2`, it receives entries and updates `@form_trace_bubble_counts` (a map)\n   - The Trace Map renders with `mode: :filter` (using TraceMapBubble) but the bubbles are NOT clickable for filtering — they're read-only, just showing the live shape of traffic\n   - (Alternative: `mode: :readonly` or `:view` — add to TraceMapBubble's mode allowlist, rendering bubbles that are not clickable at all)\n\n3. **Exclusive Scope Implementation:**\n   - **Option A (recommended):** Add `exclusive_scope` as a new nullable JSONB column to `war_room_sessions`, separate from `scope`. `scope` remains a string (the inclusive scope: \"all\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", or NULL). `exclusive_scope` is an optional list `[\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/health\"]` when the operator uses the exclusive mode.\n   - **Option B:** Change `scope` to a JSONB map `%{\"mode\" => \"inclusive\"|\"exclusive\", \"value\" => \"...\", \"exclude\" => [...]}` — requires more refactoring of existing war_room_mode code that reads `scope` as a string.\n   - **Recommend Option A** — minimal changes to existing code, backward-compatible migration (`ADD COLUMN exclusive_scope JSONB`), and clearer semantics.\n   - In `WarRoom.check/1`, match on `exclusive_scope` being non-nil and non-empty; if present, check if the entry's `route_template` is in the list before tagging.\n\n4. **Detail View Log Streaming:**\n   - Load initial logs: `LogStore.get_logs(%{war_room_id: room_id, limit: 1000})` (or no limit, if reasonable)\n   - In `handle_info/2`, receive operational_logs entries and filter to `entry.war_room_id == @room_id` before streaming\n   - Reuse the request-stream list and detail panel components from `LogsLive` (they are already modular)\n   - The detail view is read-only — no filter controls, just a chronological thread with the same layout as `/admin/logs`'s detail panel\n\n5. **Copy-to-Markdown (AC14):**\n   - Build a Markdown string:\n     ```\n     # War Room Session {room_id}\n     \n     **Started by:** {started_by}\n     **Reason:** {reason}\n     **Scope:** {scope}\n     **Filter:** {filter}\n     **Time:** {started_at} → {ended_at}\n     **Entries collected:** {entries_collected} / {max_entries}\n     \n     ## Captured Logs\n     \n     {for each entry}\n     - `{timestamp}` [{level}] [{source}] {message}\n     ```\n   - Use a client-side library (Clipboard API via `navigator.clipboard.writeText()`) to copy to the user's clipboard\n   - Show a toast/flash message \"Copied to clipboard\" via `JS.dispatch/2` or a `handle_event/3` round-trip\n\n6. **Trace Map Mode Decision (AC8's reuse of TraceMapBubble):**\n   - The form's Trace Map shows live counts (identical to the monitoring page's) but is NOT filterable\n   - Add a new TraceMapBubble mode `:view` (read-only, no phx-click) OR reuse `:filter` mode but pass a no-op `on_click` (e.g., `JS.noop()`)\n   - Recommend `:readonly` as a new mode value in TraceMapBubble — clearly signals intent and allows the component to render a disabled/non-interactive button if needed\n\n7. **Exclusive Scope UI (AC9–AC10):**\n   - The exclude-list selector can be a set of checkboxes, one per pre-defined noisy endpoint\n   - Or, a multi-select dropdown with the same set\n   - Store the selected list in the form's `phx-value-*` attributes or in a hidden input, passed to `start_war_room` event\n   - In the LiveView, validate that the provided list contains only known, safe route_templates before inserting into the DB (defense-in-depth: do not trust the form's UI validation alone)\n\n### Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Unit tests (scope_selector component):** Render with different scope modes, verify the conditional UI.\n2. **Integration tests (WarRoomListLive & WarRoomDetailLive):** Mount the views, verify session loading, live Trace Map updates, form submission, navigation, 404 handling.\n3. **Exclusive scope logic:** Test `WarRoom.check/1` with exclusive_scope set, verify that excluded entries are not tagged.\n4. **Accessibility audit:** Verify status badges, button states, nav items are all semantic.\n5. **E2E test (optional):** A Playwright scenario that navigates to the War Room list, starts a session, verifies it appears in the list, clicks it, views the detail page, and copies the markdown.\n\n### Design Decision: Generalize to \"Noisy Endpoint Exclusion\" (AC11)\n\nIf AC11 is in scope (generalize exclusion to the monitoring page itself):\n- Add a new `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.log_view_excluded_endpoints/0` helper that reads from `server_config` or a default list\n- Call it in `LogsLive.mount/3` to seed `@excluded_endpoints`\n- In the monitoring page's Trace Map rendering, filter out bubbles/entries for excluded endpoints\n- In the War Room form, show the same list by default (read from config)\n- Make it configurable via `/admin/config` (a new field in ConfigLive, added to `server_config`)\n\nIf AC11 is out of scope:\n- Document in Dev Notes that this is a follow-up opportunity and note where the code would live\n\n## Tasks / Subtasks\n\nSuperseded by the \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\" section — the dev-story implemented the AMENDED design (one LiveView module, not three files) rather than the stale original plan below. Checked off against what was actually built:\n\n- [x] Create migration: `server/priv/migrations/000021_war_room_exclusive_scope.{up,down}.sql` — `ALTER TABLE war_room_sessions ADD COLUMN exclusive_scope JSONB` (nullable)\n- [x] ~~Create `war_room_list_live.ex`~~ / ~~`war_room_detail_live.ex`~~ / ~~`scope_selector.ex`~~ — superseded by amendment 1: ONE module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` (`:index`/`:show` actions, no `handle_params/3`), mirroring `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own single-module shape. The exclusive-scope checkboxes are inline in the same module's form render, not a separate component.\n- [x] Update `war_room_manager.ex` to accept and validate `exclusive_scope` parameter (shape validation, Dev Notes item 7)\n- [x] Update `log_store/war_room.ex` to handle exclusive scope in `check/1`\n- [x] Update `router.ex` — repoints the former `/logs/war-room` stub (amendment 1), adds `/logs/war-room/:room_id`\n- [x] Update `layouts.ex` — sidebar recording dot + topbar recording pill (amendment 4); the \"War Room\" nav child already existed from `admin-sidebar-nesting`\n- [x] Update `logs_live.ex` — embedded War Room panel REMOVED (amendment 2), not a link added; war-room status poll kept for the sidebar dot/topbar pill\n- [x] Write acceptance tests FIRST (ATDD) — already staged before this dev-story cycle\n- [x] Implement `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` (list mode + detail mode)\n- [x] Verify all tests pass (unit suite 1557/1557; new-story integration suite verified per-file, see Dev Agent Record)\n- [x] Manual accessibility audit — native `<a>`/`<button>` rows, paired emoji+text badges, `aria-live` copy toast; deferred full audit to Kassandra's security/UX review\n- [ ] Security review (Kassandra) — pending, `security_review: required` per frontmatter\n\n## Cross-Story Dependencies & Notes\n\n### Depends On (must be ready before this story's dev):\n- **war-room-mode** — provides the `war_room_sessions` table, `WarRoomManager` API, scope/filter matching logic, and the embedded panel UI this story extends\n- **admin-logs-monitoring-view** — provides the request-stream list and detail-panel components that this story reuses on the detail view\n- **operational-log-store** — provides the `operational_logs` table and the `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub topic for live updates\n\n### Coordination Notes:\n- The `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` component is reused in the form (AC8). Confirm if a new mode (`:readonly` or `:view`) is needed, or if `:filter` with a no-op `on_click` is acceptable.\n- The exclusive_scope logic in `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check/1` must be coordinated with the scope-matching design from war-room-mode. Verify that adding `exclusive_scope` does not conflict with the existing scope string matching.\n- The session list/detail LiveViews reuse components from LogsLive (request stream, detail panel). Ensure these components are properly extracted as standalone, reusable modules if they are not already.\n\n## Design Decisions\n\n### AC11 Exclusion Scope (Noisy Endpoints)\n**Decision:** This story implements exclusive scope ONLY for War Room sessions. The broader monitoring-page filtering is a follow-up. If the implementation reveals trivial generalization, it SHOULD be implemented as a bonus.\n\n### AC12 Nav Integration\n**Decision:** Add a flat nav item \"War Room\" for `/admin/logs/war-room`. If nested nav is built later (separate epic), refactor then.\n\n### Exclusive Scope Schema (AC9–AC10)\n**Decision:** Use Option A — add a separate `exclusive_scope JSONB` column to `war_room_sessions`, keeping `scope` as a string. This is backward-compatible and minimal-change.\n\n### Trace Map Mode for AC8\n**Decision:** Add a new TraceMapBubble mode `:readonly` (or reuse `:filter` with no-op `on_click`). Document clearly that the form's Trace Map is read-only, not for filtering.\n\n## Dev Agent Record\n\n### Implementation Summary (dev-story cycle 0)\n\nImplemented per the story's \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\" section (authoritative over\nthe stale original AC text): ONE LiveView module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`\n(`:index`/`:show` actions, no `handle_params/3`), repointing the former\n`/admin/logs/war-room` stub; the embedded War Room panel on `/admin/logs` was removed;\n`TraceMapBubble` gained a `:readonly` mode reused for the level+source live-activity\npreview; `exclusive_scope` was added as a nullable JSONB column (migration 000021) and\nthreaded through `WarRoomManager.start_session/1` (shape-validated, not a closed\nallowlist) and `WarRoom.check/1`; the sidebar recording dot / topbar recording pill were\nadded to `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` via a new shared `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator`\nhelper, wired into every remaining admin LiveView (`DashboardLive`, `AuditLogLive`,\n`LogsLive`, `LogsLive.TraceMap`, `LogsLive.WarRoom` — the full \"anywhere in the admin UI\"\nsurface after admin-http-refactor's controller conversions); copy-to-markdown reuses the\nexisting `nebu:copy-thread` CSP-safe client event.\n\nAn \"AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one active session\" banner (reusing the retired\nembedded panel's own `war-room-panel`/`stop-war-room-button` testids) was added to the\ndedicated page's list view, replacing the start form while a session is active — this\nwas NOT in the original story text but was required to keep the pre-existing\n`war-room.feature`/`logs-trace-map.feature` E2E scenarios' reused step definitions\n(`the operator starts/stops a War Room`) working against the new page.\n\n### Bugs found and fixed during E2E verification (outside the story's own file list, but\nrequired to make the ATDD/E2E suite genuinely green)\n\n- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: `runSqlReturningId` didn't strip psql's trailing command-tag line\n  (\"INSERT 0 1\") from its `-t -A` output, corrupting every ID it returned — fixed to take\n  only the first line.\n- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: `clearWarRoomSessions` didn't delete the referencing\n  `operational_logs` rows first, so it failed with an FK-constraint violation once any\n  scenario had attached captured entries to a session — fixed to delete children first.\n- `e2e/fixtures/db.ts`: removed a duplicate `endActiveWarRoomSessions` (already existed\n  from `trace-map-node-controls`) that this story's own staged diff re-added, which broke\n  `bddgen`'s TypeScript compile entirely (`SyntaxError: Identifier ... has already been\n  declared`).\n- `e2e/features/admin/war-room.feature`: retired (embedded panel scenarios), mirroring\n  the ExUnit-side `war_room_panel_retirement_test.exs`.\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`: its own `@ac6-war-room-banner` scenario\n  started a session via the now-retired embedded panel on `/admin/logs` — repointed to\n  start it via the new `/admin/logs/war-room` page instead (same reused step\n  definitions).\n\n### Round 2 fixes (coordinator-reported, 2026-07-08 — after a clean `war_room_sessions`\ntruncate isolated 4 real bugs from the earlier round's environmental noise)\n\n1. **Cross-scenario pollution WITHIN one suite run.** Every scenario in\n   `war-room-dedicated-page.feature` seeds a session and nothing cleaned up between\n   scenarios (only the single `@ac4` scenario's own \"no sessions exist\" step called\n   `clearWarRoomSessions`). Fixed by tagging every scenario `@war-room-dedicated-page` and\n   registering `After({ tags: '@war-room-dedicated-page' }, () => clearWarRoomSessions())`\n   in the steps file — mirrors `logs-trace-map.steps.ts`'s own `@node-controls`\n   tag-scoped-hook pattern exactly.
