Also broadened `clearWarRoomSessions`'s WHERE clause\n   (`started_by LIKE '@e2e-wr-%' OR reason LIKE 'e2e %'`) since the AC9/AC10 scenario\n   starts its session through the REAL UI, so its `started_by` is the logged-in e2e\n   admin's real id, never `@e2e-wr-%` — only its literal `reason` (\"e2e exclusive scope\n   test\") is a usable cleanup marker for that row.\n2. **Exclusive-scope options never appeared.** Root cause: NOT a template/testid bug —\n   `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`'s scope `<select>` correctly reveals\n   `war-room-exclusive-options` via a `phx-change` round trip (proven by the ExUnit\n   `render_change` test, which passed all along). The shared `war-room.steps.ts`'s\n   \"the operator selects {string} as the War Room scope\" step was missing the\n   `[data-phx-main].phx-connected` wait its own sibling \"starts a War Room...\" step already\n   has — on the throttled-longpoll transport, `selectOption()` fired before the LiveSocket\n   connected, so the native `change` event landed on a dead, unconnected static DOM and\n   the server round trip never happened. Fixed by adding the same wait.\n3. **Copy-to-markdown toast never appeared.** Same root cause as #2: \"the operator clicks\n   the copy-to-markdown button\" (`war-room-dedicated-page.steps.ts`) had no\n   `phx-connected` wait. The button's `phx-click` is\n   `JS.dispatch(\"nebu:copy-thread\") |> JS.push(\"copy_to_markdown\")` — the `dispatch` half\n   is pure client-side (fires even pre-connection, which is why the clipboard write could\n   still silently succeed) but the `push` that actually sets `@copy_toast` server-side\n   needs a live socket. Fixed by adding the wait.\n4. **Keyboard-operability scenario: `select[name=\"scope\"]` never found.** Same root cause\n   as #2 (this scenario reuses the same buggy shared step) — fixing #2 fixed this too.\n\n### Verification (final, after round 2)\n\n- `mix test --warnings-as-errors` (no `NEBU_DB_URL`, mirrors `make test-unit-elixir`):\n  **1557/1557 passed, 0 failures, exit code 0** (also fixed an unrelated pre-existing\n  `--warnings-as-errors`-fatal compile warning in `war_room_test.exs`'s\n  `insert_operational_log/2` helper — an unused default argument value — that was silently\n  aborting the run even though every individual test passed).\n- `mix test --include integration` targeted at every new/touched test file: passes\n  cleanly in isolation (91-98 tests depending on grouping).\n- E2E (`--project=admin-ui --grep \"War Room\"`): **10/10 passed** against a genuinely clean\n  `war_room_sessions` table (confirmed empty after the run via the new After-hook).\n- E2E regression sweep (`--grep \"Trace Map|Node Controls|sidebar\"`, 23 scenarios): all\n  green (one intermittent, unrelated node-overlap click flake in\n  `logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature` — pure geometry/traffic-volume timing, not\n  touched by this story — passed cleanly on retry).\n\n## Design Update (2026-07-07 — Trace Map Leitstand)\n\nPhil approved an interactive design dummy covering this story's page shell. This story's job is to\n   **replace the stub**: `live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive.WarRoom, :index` (new LiveView,\n   naming consistent with the sibling `LogsLive.TraceMap` module) with\n   `@active_nav = \"logs-war-room\"`, plus `live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\", LogsLive.WarRoom, :show`\n   for the detail view (same module, two `handle_params` shaped actions — mirror however\n   `LogsLive.TraceMap` structures its own single-LiveView, multi-concern pattern; do NOT\n   invent a second top-level nav entry).\n\n2. **Embedded panel retirement.** `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomPanel.war_room_panel/1` is\n   STILL embedded on `/admin/logs` (Live Stream page, `logs_live.ex:1007`) — this story's\n   own premise (\"dedicated page ... rather than only an embedded panel\") means it must be\n   REMOVED from Live Stream once the dedicated page ships (mirrors exactly how\n   `trace-map-leitstand` retired the embedded Trace Map from that same page). The\n   \"start a new session\" form (AC7/AC8) moves to the new page entirely. `WarRoomPanel`'s\n   status-badge/warning sub-components may be reused by the new page if convenient; the\n   component itself no longer renders on Live Stream.\n\n3. **AC8's Trace Map reuse — `TraceMapBubble`, NOT the new radial Leitstand map.**\n   `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` (level/source dimensions) is unchanged and still\n   live on `/admin/logs`; only the ENDPOINT dimension moved to the new radial\n   `LogsLive.TraceMap` page. Toast/flash via a\n   `handle_event` round-trip is fine and matches the rest of the admin UI.\n\n7. **Security review stays REQUIRED** (already set in frontmatter) — this story writes\n   admin-supplied exclusive-scope lists into session records and exposes historical log\n   content on the detail page; {"parentUuid":"b2b5a836-6aa9-4417-af72-cea687b8713e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SzXrUmMcvQ1x5JARQX6cBM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  In-memory cache + atomicity boundary for the SINGLE active War Room\n4\t  session (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode, AC4/AC5/AC9).\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n7\t  Design decisions (see #3/#4 for the full rationale behind this module's\n8\t  existence and its \"own inline SQL\" ownership):\n9\t  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n10\t\n11\t  ## Why `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.log/2` calls this, not\n12\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer`\n13\t\n14\t  The Writer batches up to 100 entries (or flushes every 5s) — AC4/AC9's\n15\t  \"the Nth matching entry ends the session; the N+1th is not tagged\" needs\n16\t  single-entry granularity, and `Handler.log/2` only ever calls\n17\t  `Writer.write_async/1` when `LogStore.should_persist?/1` is already\n18\t  `true`. AC4 explicitly requires war-room-matched entries to persist\n19\t  REGARDLESS of the ambient per-level retention policy (e.g. a DEBUG entry\n20\t  when `log_collect_debug` is `false`) — such an entry would never even\n21\t  reach the Writer if the check lived there. So the match/tag/budget-check\n22\t  decision happens HERE, synchronously, called from `Handler.log/2` BEFORE\n23\t  its `should_persist?/1` gate.\n24\t\n25\t  ## Hot-path cost when inactive (Oracle gate — MUST NOT delay a `/sync`\n26\t  response, or any other request)\n27\t\n28\t  `check/1`'s fast path is a single `:persistent_term.get/2` read — no\n29\t  GenServer round-trip at all — whenever no war room session is currently\n30\t  cached as active. This mirrors `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache`'s own\n31\t  \"never touch the DB / never block this hot path when nothing is going on\"\n32\t  philosophy. Only when a session genuinely IS active does `check/1`\n33\t  perform a `GenServer.call/2` — necessarily synchronous, since the\n34\t  increment-and-maybe-terminate step must be serialized through ONE process\n35\t  to be atomic (see below), and a war room's own `max_entries` cap (at most\n36\t  10 000, the feature's own safety bound) keeps this call volume bounded by\n37\t  design.\n38\t\n39\t  ## Atomicity — the GenServer itself is the boundary, not a DB transaction\n40\t\n41\t  Every `check/1` call for the currently-active session is serialized\n42\t  through this SAME GenServer process. The single-row, single-statement SQL\n43\t  `UPDATE ... SET entries_collected = entries_collected + 1[, ended_at =\n44\t  now(), ended_reason = 'budget_exhausted']` conditionally ends the session\n45\t  in the SAME statement that increments the counter — genuinely atomic, no\n46\t  read-then-write race — and because it is only ever issued from inside\n47\t  this one process's `handle_call/3`, there is no cross-process race on the\n48\t  counter either: the in-memory `state.session` flips to `nil` (and the\n49\t  `:persistent_term` flag flips to `false`) in the very same `handle_call`\n50\t  that pushed the count past `max_entries`, before the call returns to the\n51\t  caller. This is what makes AT9's \"the 6th matching entry is NOT tagged\"\n52\t  hold.\n53\t\n54\t  ## Redaction — no new leak path\n55\t\n56\t  This module only ever reads/writes `route_template`, `metadata[\"user_id\"\n57\t  / \"device_id\"]`, and `war_room_id` — never `message` or any other\n58\t  content field. The two-category redaction from\n59\t  `matrix-payload-debug-logging` (tokens/credentials + chat message\n60\t  content) happens entirely upstream, in `RequestLogger`, before an entry\n61\t  ever reaches `Handler.log/2`; this module never re-derives or touches\n62\t  that already-redacted content.\n63\t  \"\"\"\n64\t\n65\t  use GenServer\n66\t\n67\t  require Logger\n68\t\n69\t  @active_flag_key :nebu_war_room_active_flag\n70\t\n71\t  def start_link(_opts \\\\ []), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)\n72\t\n73\t  @doc \"\"\"\n74\t  Checks `entry` (the same map shape `Handler.parse_log_event/1` produces —\n75\t  atom keys `:level`/`:source`/`:route_template`/`:request_id`/`:message`/\n76\t  `:metadata`, where `:metadata` has STRING keys) against the currently\n77\t  active war room session's scope/filter.\n78\t\n79\t  Returns the active session's `id` (a UUID string) when `entry` matches —\n80\t  the caller (`Handler.log/2`) is expected to tag the entry with it — or\n81\t  `nil` when no session is active, or the session is active but `entry`\n82\t  does not match its scope/filter.\n83\t\n84\t  Cheap and non-blocking when inactive (see moduledoc). Never raises: a\n85\t  crashed/not-yet-started `WarRoom` process (e.g. mid-restart after a\n86\t  crash) is treated the same as \"no active session\" — logging must never\n87\t  turn into a request failure (Oracle gate).\n88\t  \"\"\"\n89\t  @spec check(map()) :: nil | String.t()\n90\t  def check(entry) do\n91\t    if :persistent_term.get(@active_flag_key, false) do\n92\t      try do\n93\t        GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:check, entry})\n94\t      catch\n95\t        :exit, _ -> nil\n96\t      end\n97\t    else\n98\t      nil\n99\t    end\n100\t  end\n101\t\n102\t  @doc \"\"\"\n103\t  (Re)loads the single active session (`ended_at IS NULL`) from the DB into\n104\t  this process's cache — called at boot (`init/1`) and after every\n105\t  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1` / `stop_session/1` write\n106\t  (design-decisions.md #4), mirroring\n107\t  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0`.\n108\t\n109\t  If the loaded session is already past its `max_duration_s` deadline\n110\t  (e.g. this process was down past the deadline and has just restarted —\n111\t  including via a crash+supervisor-restart), it is ended immediately as\n112\t  `\"duration_exceeded\"` right here, rather than left dangling until some\n113\t  future timer fires — AC5 applies across a restart, not only while this\n114\t  process stays up continuously.\n115\t\n116\t  Safe no-op (returns `:ok`) when this process is not currently running.\n117\t  \"\"\"\n118\t  @spec load_active() :: :ok\n119\t  def load_active do\n120\t    case Process.whereis(__MODULE__) do\n121\t      nil -> :ok\n122\t      pid -> GenServer.call(pid, :load_active)\n123\t    end\n124\t  end\n125\t\n126\t  # ── GenServer callbacks ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n127\t\n128\t  @impl true\n129\t  def init(:ok) do\n130\t    {:ok, do_load_active(%{session: nil, timer_ref: nil})}\n131\t  end\n132\t\n133\t  @impl true\n134\t  def handle_call(:load_active, _from, state) do\n135\t    new_state = state |> cancel_timer() |> do_load_active()\n136\t    {:reply, :ok, new_state}\n137\t  end\n138\t\n139\t  def handle_call({:check, _entry}, _from, %{session: nil} = state) do\n140\t    {:reply, nil, state}\n141\t  end\n142\t\n143\t  def handle_call({:check, entry}, _from, %{session: session} = state) do\n144\t    if matches?(session, entry) do\n145\t      case increment_and_maybe_end(session) do\n146\t        {:ok, true, _new_count} ->\n147\t          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n148\t          {:reply, session.id, cancel_timer(%{state | session: nil})}\n149\t\n150\t        {:ok, false, new_count} ->\n151\t          {:reply, session.id, %{state | session: %{session | entries_collected: new_count}}}\n152\t\n153\t        # Defensive: the cached session's row is no longer in the DB at all\n154\t        # (e.g. a hard DELETE, not the normal ended_at UPDATE path this\n155\t        # module always uses itself — observed in practice from test\n156\t        # cleanup racing this singleton, cross-process, global-name\n157\t        # GenServer). Never crash the hot Handler.log/2 path over this —\n158\t        # simply treat it the same as \"no session active\" from here on.\n159\t        :gone ->\n160\t          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n161\t          {:reply, nil, cancel_timer(%{state | session: nil})}\n162\t      end\n163\t    else\n164\t      {:reply, nil, state}\n165\t    end\n166\t  end\n167\t\n168\t  @impl true\n169\t  def handle_info({:check_timeout, id}, %{session: %{id: id} = session} = state) do\n170\t    if past_deadline?(session) do\n171\t      end_session(session.id, \"duration_exceeded\")\n172\t      :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n173\t      {:noreply, %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}}\n174\t    else\n175\t      # The timer fired slightly early (should not normally happen —\n176\t      # schedule_timeout/1 computes the remaining time exactly) — reschedule\n177\t      # defensively rather than dropping the check entirely.\n178\t      {:noreply, %{state | timer_ref: schedule_timeout(session)}}\n179\t    end\n180\t  end\n181\t\n182\t  # A timer message for a session that is no longer the currently-cached one\n183\t  # (e.g. a stale message left in-flight from a fast stop+immediate-restart\n184\t  # — design-decisions.md #4) — ignored.\n185\t  def handle_info({:check_timeout, _stale_id}, state), do: {:noreply, state}\n186\t  def handle_info(_other, state), do: {:noreply, state}\n187\t\n188\t  # ── loading ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n189\t\n190\t  defp do_load_active(state) do\n191\t    case fetch_active_row() do\n192\t      nil ->\n193\t        :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n194\t        %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n195\t\n196\t      session ->\n197\t        if past_deadline?(session) do\n198\t          end_session(session.id, \"duration_exceeded\")\n199\t          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n200\t          %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n201\t        else\n202\t          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, true)\n203\t          %{state | session: session, timer_ref: schedule_timeout(session)}\n204\t        end\n205\t    end\n206\t  rescue\n207\t    e ->\n208\t      Logger.warning(\"Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom failed to load active session: #{inspect(e)}\")\n209\t      :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n210\t      %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n211\t  end\n212\t\n213\t  defp cancel_timer(%{timer_ref: nil} = state), do: state\n214\t\n215\t  defp cancel_timer(%{timer_ref: ref} = state) do\n216\t    Process.cancel_timer(ref)\n217\t    %{state | timer_ref: nil}\n218\t  end\n219\t\n220\t  defp schedule_timeout(session) do\n221\t    elapsed_ms = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), session.started_at, :millisecond)\n222\t    remaining_ms = max(0, session.max_duration_s * 1_000 - elapsed_ms)\n223\t    Process.send_after(self(), {:check_timeout, session.id}, remaining_ms)\n224\t  end\n225\t\n226\t  defp past_deadline?(session) do\n227\t    elapsed_s = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), session.started_at, :second)\n228\t    elapsed_s >= session.max_duration_s\n229\t  end\n230\t\n231\t  # ── scope/filter matching (AC3/AC9) ──────────────────────────────────────\n232\t\n233\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (AC9/AC10): the exclusion check\n234\t  # runs BEFORE, and independently of, scope/filter matching — an excluded\n235\t  # route_template is skipped even when `scope: \"all\"` would otherwise match\n236\t  # everything, and even when `filter` would otherwise match the entry's\n237\t  # metadata exactly (AC10's literal \"if yes, skip — do not tag, do not\n238\t  # count\"). `exclusive_scope: nil`/`[]` is a pure no-op, 100% backward\n239\t  # compatible with every pre-existing scope/filter test in this module.\n240\t  defp matches?(session, entry) do\n241\t    not excluded?(Map.get(session, :exclusive_scope), entry[:route_template]) and\n242\t      scope_matches?(session.scope, entry[:route_template]) and\n243\t      filter_matches?(session.filter, entry[:metadata])\n244\t  end\n245\t\n246\t  defp excluded?(nil, _route), do: false\n247\t  defp excluded?([], _route), do: false\n248\t  defp excluded?(exclusive_scope, route) when is_list(exclusive_scope), do: route in exclusive_scope\n249\t  defp excluded?(_exclusive_scope, _route), do: false\n250\t\n251\t  # scope = \"all\" or NULL/nil -> always match (AC3).\n252\t  defp scope_matches?(nil, _route), do: true\n253\t  defp scope_matches?(\"all\", _route), do: true\n254\t  # A specific route_template -> exact string match only (AC3/AC8; the\n255\t  # Oracle gate confirms this codebase has exactly one /sync route, so exact\n256\t  # matching against \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\" is complete).\n257\t  defp scope_matches?(scope, route) when is_binary(scope), do: scope == route\n258\t  defp scope_matches?(_scope, _route), do: false\n259\t\n260\t  # filter = NULL/empty -> always match (no additional filtering).\n261\t  defp filter_matches?(nil, _meta), do: true\n262\t  defp filter_matches?(filter, _meta) when filter == %{}, do: true\n263\t\n264\t  # AND semantics (AC9): every filter field must be PRESENT and EQUAL in the\n265\t  # entry's metadata. `device_id` matching is opaque/case-sensitive\n266\t  # (Oracle gate) — plain `==` never lowercases/normalizes either side.\n267\t  defp filter_matches?(filter, meta) when is_map(filter) do\n268\t    meta = meta || %{}\n269\t    Enum.all?(filter, fn {k, v} -> Map.get(meta, k) == v end)\n270\t  end\n271\t\n272\t  defp filter_matches?(_filter, _meta), do: false\n273\t\n274\t  # ── DB access (this module owns its own inline SQL — design-decisions.md\n275\t  # #4 — for the load/increment/timeout paths, ADR-018-compliant) ──────────\n276\t\n277\t  defp fetch_active_row do\n278\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n279\t      result =\n280\t        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n281\t          Nebu.Repo,\n282\t          \"\"\"\n283\t          SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries,\n284\t                 max_duration_s, started_at, entries_collected\n285\t          FROM war_room_sessions\n286\t          WHERE ended_at IS NULL\n287\t          LIMIT 1\n288\t          \"\"\",\n289\t          []\n290\t        )\n291\t\n292\t      case result.rows do\n293\t        [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n294\t        [] -> nil\n295\t      end\n296\t    else\n297\t      nil\n298\t    end\n299\t  end\n300\t\n301\t  defp row_to_session([\n302\t         id,\n303\t         started_by,\n304\t         reason,\n305\t         scope,\n306\t         filter,\n307\t         exclusive_scope,\n308\t         max_entries,\n309\t         max_duration_s,\n310\t         started_at,\n311\t         entries_collected\n312\t       ]) do\n313\t    %{\n314\t      id: id,\n315\t      started_by: started_by,\n316\t      reason: reason,\n317\t      scope: scope,\n318\t      filter: filter,\n319\t      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n320\t      max_entries: max_entries,\n321\t      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n322\t      started_at: started_at,\n323\t      entries_collected: entries_collected\n324\t    }\n325\t  end\n326\t\n327\t  # Single, atomic UPDATE ... RETURNING — increments the counter AND\n328\t  # conditionally ends the session in the SAME statement (no read-then-write\n329\t  # race). Returns {:ok, ended?, new_entries_collected}, or `:gone` when the\n330\t  # row no longer exists at all (defensive — this module never deletes\n331\t  # rows itself; see the `:gone` handling in handle_call/3 above).\n332\t  defp increment_and_maybe_end(session) do\n333\t    result =\n334\t      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n335\t        Nebu.Repo,\n336\t        \"\"\"\n337\t        UPDATE war_room_sessions\n338\t        SET entries_collected = entries_collected + 1,\n339\t            ended_at = CASE\n340\t              WHEN entries_collected + 1 >= max_entries THEN now()\n341\t              ELSE ended_at\n342\t            END,\n343\t            ended_reason = CASE\n344\t              WHEN entries_collected + 1 >= max_entries THEN 'budget_exhausted'\n345\t              ELSE ended_reason\n346\t            END\n347\t        WHERE id = $1\n348\t        RETURNING entries_collected, ended_at\n349\t        \"\"\",\n350\t        [session.id]\n351\t      )\n352\t\n353\t    case result.rows do\n354\t      [[new_count, ended_at]] -> {:ok, not is_nil(ended_at), new_count}\n355\t      [] -> :gone\n356\t    end\n357\t  end\n358\t\n359\t  defp end_session(id, reason) do\n360\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n361\t      Nebu.Repo,\n362\t      \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = $2 WHERE id = $1 AND ended_at IS NULL\",\n363\t      [id, reason]\n364\t    )\n365\t\n366\t    :ok\n367\t  rescue\n368\t    e ->\n369\t      Logger.warning(\"Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom failed to end session #{id}: #{inspect(e)}\")\n370\t      :ok\n371\t  end\n372\tend\n373\t"}]},"uuid":"b44522b0-be07-429f-ab8d-387696495daf","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:19:37.623Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/war_room.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  In-memory cache + atomicity boundary for the SINGLE active War Room\n  session (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode, AC4/AC5/AC9).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n  Design decisions (see #3/#4 for the full rationale behind this module's\n  existence and its \"own inline SQL\" ownership):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n\n  ## Why `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.log/2` calls this, not\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer`\n\n  The Writer batches up to 100 entries (or flushes every 5s) — AC4/AC9's\n  \"the Nth matching entry ends the session; the N+1th is not tagged\" needs\n  single-entry granularity, and `Handler.log/2` only ever calls\n  `Writer.write_async/1` when `LogStore.should_persist?/1` is already\n  `true`. AC4 explicitly requires war-room-matched entries to persist\n  REGARDLESS of the ambient per-level retention policy (e.g. a DEBUG entry\n  when `log_collect_debug` is `false`) — such an entry would never even\n  reach the Writer if the check lived there. So the match/tag/budget-check\n  decision happens HERE, synchronously, called from `Handler.log/2` BEFORE\n  its `should_persist?/1` gate.\n\n  ## Hot-path cost when inactive (Oracle gate — MUST NOT delay a `/sync`\n  response, or any other request)\n\n  `check/1`'s fast path is a single `:persistent_term.get/2` read — no\n  GenServer round-trip at all — whenever no war room session is currently\n  cached as active. This mirrors `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache`'s own\n  \"never touch the DB / never block this hot path when nothing is going on\"\n  philosophy. Only when a session genuinely IS active does `check/1`\n  perform a `GenServer.call/2` — necessarily synchronous, since the\n  increment-and-maybe-terminate step must be serialized through ONE process\n  to be atomic (see below), and a war room's own `max_entries` cap (at most\n  10 000, the feature's own safety bound) keeps this call volume bounded by\n  design.\n\n  ## Atomicity — the GenServer itself is the boundary, not a DB transaction\n\n  Every `check/1` call for the currently-active session is serialized\n  through this SAME GenServer process. The single-row, single-statement SQL\n  `UPDATE ... SET entries_collected = entries_collected + 1[, ended_at =\n  now(), ended_reason = 'budget_exhausted']` conditionally ends the session\n  in the SAME statement that increments the counter — genuinely atomic, no\n  read-then-write race — and because it is only ever issued from inside\n  this one process's `handle_call/3`, there is no cross-process race on the\n  counter either: the in-memory `state.session` flips to `nil` (and the\n  `:persistent_term` flag flips to `false`) in the very same `handle_call`\n  that pushed the count past `max_entries`, before the call returns to the\n  caller. This is what makes AT9's \"the 6th matching entry is NOT tagged\"\n  hold.\n\n  ## Redaction — no new leak path\n\n  This module only ever reads/writes `route_template`, `metadata[\"user_id\"\n  / \"device_id\"]`, and `war_room_id` — never `message` or any other\n  content field. The two-category redaction from\n  `matrix-payload-debug-logging` (tokens/credentials + chat message\n  content) happens entirely upstream, in `RequestLogger`, before an entry\n  ever reaches `Handler.log/2`; this module never re-derives or touches\n  that already-redacted content.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use GenServer\n\n  require Logger\n\n  @active_flag_key :nebu_war_room_active_flag\n\n  def start_link(_opts \\\\ []), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Checks `entry` (the same map shape `Handler.parse_log_event/1` produces —\n  atom keys `:level`/`:source`/`:route_template`/`:request_id`/`:message`/\n  `:metadata`, where `:metadata` has STRING keys) against the currently\n  active war room session's scope/filter.\n\n  Returns the active session's `id` (a UUID string) when `entry` matches —\n  the caller (`Handler.log/2`) is expected to tag the entry with it — or\n  `nil` when no session is active, or the session is active but `entry`\n  does not match its scope/filter.\n\n  Cheap and non-blocking when inactive (see moduledoc). Never raises: a\n  crashed/not-yet-started `WarRoom` process (e.g. mid-restart after a\n  crash) is treated the same as \"no active session\" — logging must never\n  turn into a request failure (Oracle gate).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec check(map()) :: nil | String.t()\n  def check(entry) do\n    if :persistent_term.get(@active_flag_key, false) do\n      try do\n        GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:check, entry})\n      catch\n        :exit, _ -> nil\n      end\n    else\n      nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  (Re)loads the single active session (`ended_at IS NULL`) from the DB into\n  this process's cache — called at boot (`init/1`) and after every\n  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1` / `stop_session/1` write\n  (design-decisions.md #4), mirroring\n  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0`.\n\n  If the loaded session is already past its `max_duration_s` deadline\n  (e.g. this process was down past the deadline and has just restarted —\n  including via a crash+supervisor-restart), it is ended immediately as\n  `\"duration_exceeded\"` right here, rather than left dangling until some\n  future timer fires — AC5 applies across a restart, not only while this\n  process stays up continuously.\n\n  Safe no-op (returns `:ok`) when this process is not currently running.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec load_active() :: :ok\n  def load_active do\n    case Process.whereis(__MODULE__) do\n      nil -> :ok\n      pid -> GenServer.call(pid, :load_active)\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ── GenServer callbacks ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  @impl true\n  def init(:ok) do\n    {:ok, do_load_active(%{session: nil, timer_ref: nil})}\n  end\n\n  @impl true\n  def handle_call(:load_active, _from, state) do\n    new_state = state |> cancel_timer() |> do_load_active()\n    {:reply, :ok, new_state}\n  end\n\n  def handle_call({:check, _entry}, _from, %{session: nil} = state) do\n    {:reply, nil, state}\n  end\n\n  def handle_call({:check, entry}, _from, %{session: session} = state) do\n    if matches?(session, entry) do\n      case increment_and_maybe_end(session) do\n        {:ok, true, _new_count} ->\n          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n          {:reply, session.id, cancel_timer(%{state | session: nil})}\n\n        {:ok, false, new_count} ->\n          {:reply, session.id, %{state | session: %{session | entries_collected: new_count}}}\n\n        # Defensive: the cached session's row is no longer in the DB at all\n        # (e.g. a hard DELETE, not the normal ended_at UPDATE path this\n        # module always uses itself — observed in practice from test\n        # cleanup racing this singleton, cross-process, global-name\n        # GenServer). Never crash the hot Handler.log/2 path over this —\n        # simply treat it the same as \"no session active\" from here on.\n        :gone ->\n          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n          {:reply, nil, cancel_timer(%{state | session: nil})}\n      end\n    else\n      {:reply, nil, state}\n    end\n  end\n\n  @impl true\n  def handle_info({:check_timeout, id}, %{session: %{id: id} = session} = state) do\n    if past_deadline?(session) do\n      end_session(session.id, \"duration_exceeded\")\n      :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n      {:noreply, %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}}\n    else\n      # The timer fired slightly early (should not normally happen —\n      # schedule_timeout/1 computes the remaining time exactly) — reschedule\n      # defensively rather than dropping the check entirely.\n      {:noreply, %{state | timer_ref: schedule_timeout(session)}}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # A timer message for a session that is no longer the currently-cached one\n  # (e.g. a stale message left in-flight from a fast stop+immediate-restart\n  # — design-decisions.md #4) — ignored.\n  def handle_info({:check_timeout, _stale_id}, state), do: {:noreply, state}\n  def handle_info(_other, state), do: {:noreply, state}\n\n  # ── loading ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp do_load_active(state) do\n    case fetch_active_row() do\n      nil ->\n        :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n        %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n\n      session ->\n        if past_deadline?(session) do\n          end_session(session.id, \"duration_exceeded\")\n          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n          %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n        else\n          :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, true)\n          %{state | session: session, timer_ref: schedule_timeout(session)}\n        end\n    end\n  rescue\n    e ->\n      Logger.warning(\"Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom failed to load active session: #{inspect(e)}\")\n      :persistent_term.put(@active_flag_key, false)\n      %{state | session: nil, timer_ref: nil}\n  end\n\n  defp cancel_timer(%{timer_ref: nil} = state), do: state\n\n  defp cancel_timer(%{timer_ref: ref} = state) do\n    Process.cancel_timer(ref)\n    %{state | timer_ref: nil}\n  end\n\n  defp schedule_timeout(session) do\n    elapsed_ms = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), session.started_at, :millisecond)\n    remaining_ms = max(0, session.max_duration_s * 1_000 - elapsed_ms)\n    Process.send_after(self(), {:check_timeout, session.id}, remaining_ms)\n  end\n\n  defp past_deadline?(session) do\n    elapsed_s = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), session.started_at, :second)\n    elapsed_s >= session.max_duration_s\n  end\n\n  # ── scope/filter matching (AC3/AC9) ──────────────────────────────────────\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (AC9/AC10): the exclusion check\n  # runs BEFORE, and independently of, scope/filter matching — an excluded\n  # route_template is skipped even when `scope: \"all\"` would otherwise match\n  # everything, and even when `filter` would otherwise match the entry's\n  # metadata exactly (AC10's literal \"if yes, skip — do not tag, do not\n  # count\"). `exclusive_scope: nil`/`[]` is a pure no-op, 100% backward\n  # compatible with every pre-existing scope/filter test in this module.\n  defp matches?(session, entry) do\n    not excluded?(Map.get(session, :exclusive_scope), entry[:route_template]) and\n      scope_matches?(session.scope, entry[:route_template]) and\n      filter_matches?(session.filter, entry[:metadata])\n  end\n\n  defp excluded?(nil, _route), do: false\n  defp excluded?([], _route), do: false\n  defp excluded?(exclusive_scope, route) when is_list(exclusive_scope), do: route in exclusive_scope\n  defp excluded?(_exclusive_scope, _route), do: false\n\n  # scope = \"all\" or NULL/nil -> always match (AC3).\n  defp scope_matches?(nil, _route), do: true\n  defp scope_matches?(\"all\", _route), do: true\n  # A specific route_template -> exact string match only (AC3/AC8; the\n  # Oracle gate confirms this codebase has exactly one /sync route, so exact\n  # matching against \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\" is complete).\n  defp scope_matches?(scope, route) when is_binary(scope), do: scope == route\n  defp scope_matches?(_scope, _route), do: false\n\n  # filter = NULL/empty -> always match (no additional filtering).\n  defp filter_matches?(nil, _meta), do: true\n  defp filter_matches?(filter, _meta) when filter == %{}, do: true\n\n  # AND semantics (AC9): every filter field must be PRESENT and EQUAL in the\n  # entry's metadata. `device_id` matching is opaque/case-sensitive\n  # (Oracle gate) — plain `==` never lowercases/normalizes either side.\n  defp filter_matches?(filter, meta) when is_map(filter) do\n    meta = meta || %{}\n    Enum.all?(filter, fn {k, v} -> Map.get(meta, k) == v end)\n  end\n\n  defp filter_matches?(_filter, _meta), do: false\n\n  # ── DB access (this module owns its own inline SQL — design-decisions.md\n  # #4 — for the load/increment/timeout paths, ADR-018-compliant) ──────────\n\n  defp fetch_active_row do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"\"\"\n          SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries,\n                 max_duration_s, started_at, entries_collected\n          FROM war_room_sessions\n          WHERE ended_at IS NULL\n          LIMIT 1\n          \"\"\",\n          []\n        )\n\n      case result.rows do\n        [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n        [] -> nil\n      end\n    else\n      nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp row_to_session([\n         id,\n         started_by,\n         reason,\n         scope,\n         filter,\n         exclusive_scope,\n         max_entries,\n         max_duration_s,\n         started_at,\n         entries_collected\n       ]) do\n    %{\n      id: id,\n      started_by: started_by,\n      reason: reason,\n      scope: scope,\n      filter: filter,\n      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n      max_entries: max_entries,\n      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n      started_at: started_at,\n      entries_collected: entries_collected\n    }\n  end\n\n  # Single, atomic UPDATE ... RETURNING — increments the counter AND\n  # conditionally ends the session in the SAME statement (no read-then-write\n  # race). Returns {:ok, ended?, new_entries_collected}, or `:gone` when the\n  # row no longer exists at all (defensive — this module never deletes\n  # rows itself; see the `:gone` handling in handle_call/3 above).\n  defp increment_and_maybe_end(session) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        UPDATE war_room_sessions\n        SET entries_collected = entries_collected + 1,\n            ended_at = CASE\n              WHEN entries_collected + 1 >= max_entries THEN now()\n              ELSE ended_at\n            END,\n            ended_reason = CASE\n              WHEN entries_collected + 1 >= max_entries THEN 'budget_exhausted'\n              ELSE ended_reason\n            END\n        WHERE id = $1\n        RETURNING entries_collected, ended_at\n        \"\"\",\n        [session.id]\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [[new_count, ended_at]] -> {:ok, not is_nil(ended_at), new_count}\n      [] -> :gone\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp end_session(id, reason) do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = $2 WHERE id = $1 AND ended_at IS NULL\",\n      [id, reason]\n    )\n\n    :ok\n  rescue\n    e ->\n      Logger.warning(\"Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom failed to end session #{id}: #{inspect(e)}\")\n      :ok\n  end\nend\n","numLines":373,"startLine":1,"totalLines":373}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b2b5a836-6aa9-4417-af72-cea687b8713e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"694f46d1-de23-4285-a2a2-dce35280734d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N1TEFGquVqzALhaq1CSovb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Public API for War Room session lifecycle (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode,\n4\t  AC1/AC2/AC3/AC6/AC10) — `start_session/1`, `stop_session/1`, `status/0`.\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n7\t  Design decisions (see #6 for why there is deliberately NO HTTP\n8\t  controller/route here):\n9\t  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n10\t\n11\t  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — no\n12\t  `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge at all, directly unit-testable without an\n13\t  HTTP round trip. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `handle_event(\"start_war_room\" /\n14\t  \"stop_war_room\", ...)` clauses call straight into these functions.\n15\t\n16\t  ## AC2 — \"exactly one active session\"\n17\t\n18\t  This module performs the app-level \"is one already active?\" check first\n19\t  (to return a friendly, human-readable conflict message naming the\n20\t  existing session's `started_by`/`reason`), but the REAL correctness\n21\t  backstop is the migration's partial unique index\n22\t  (`war_room_sessions_single_active_idx`) — a genuinely raced concurrent\n23\t  INSERT still fails there and is mapped to the same `{:error, :conflict,\n24\t  _}` shape.\n25\t\n26\t  Restarting the SAME session (identical `started_by`/`reason`/`scope`/\n27\t  `filter`/`max_entries`/`max_duration_s`) while it is still active is\n28\t  treated as idempotent: `{:ok, existing_session}` is returned without a\n29\t  second row being created.\n30\t\n31\t  ## Cache invalidation\n32\t\n33\t  Every write here (`start_session/1`'s successful insert, `stop_session/1`)\n34\t  calls `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` afterward — mirroring\n35\t  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0` —\n36\t  so the hot-path cache (used by `Handler.log/2` via `WarRoom.check/1`)\n37\t  reflects the change on the very next log entry, not after some future\n38\t  poll.\n39\t  \"\"\"\n40\t\n41\t  @required_params [:started_by, :reason, :max_entries, :max_duration_s]\n42\t\n43\t  # Security review (Kassandra, 2026-07-06, F-1 MEDIUM): these are the SAME\n44\t  # bounds `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `parse_int_bounded/5` already enforces at\n45\t  # the UI layer (AC8/AC11) — duplicated here so the actual trust boundary\n46\t  # (this module's public API, the only thing a caller MUST go through to\n47\t  # create a session) enforces the flooding-safety invariant regardless of\n48\t  # caller, not only when going through the LiveView form. This is\n49\t  # defense-in-depth, the same pattern already used for AC2's \"exactly one\n50\t  # active\" (app-level check + DB-level partial unique index backstop) — the\n51\t  # LiveView's own bounds check stays in place as the friendly\n52\t  # early-UI-feedback layer and is NOT removed/weakened by this.\n53\t  @max_entries_bounds {10, 10_000}\n54\t  @max_duration_s_bounds {10, 3_600}\n55\t\n56\t  @doc \"\"\"\n57\t  Starts a new War Room session with `params` (a map with atom keys):\n58\t\n59\t    * required: `:started_by`, `:reason`, `:max_entries`, `:max_duration_s`\n60\t    * optional: `:scope` (defaults to `nil`, treated as \"all\"), `:filter`\n61\t      (a map, e.g. `%{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}`, defaults to `nil`),\n62\t      `:exclusive_scope` (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page, AC9/AC10\n63\t      — a list of route_template strings to EXCLUDE from capture, defaults\n64\t      to `nil`)\n65\t\n66\t  Returns:\n67\t\n68\t    * `{:ok, session}` — a freshly created session, OR the existing active\n69\t      session when `params` exactly matches it (idempotent restart, AC2).\n70\t    * `{:error, :conflict, message}` — a DIFFERENT session is already\n71\t      active (AC2).\n72\t    * `{:error, :invalid_params, message}` — a required parameter is\n73\t      missing (AC3 — no acceptance test pins the exact shape of this error;\n74\t      this is the chosen, documented contract), OR `:max_entries`/\n75\t      `:max_duration_s` is outside the AC8/AC11 bounds (`10..10_000` /\n76\t      `10..3_600` — security review F-1: this is the actual trust boundary,\n77\t      not just the LiveView form's own bounds check), OR `:exclusive_scope`\n78\t      fails its defense-in-depth shape validation (see\n79\t      `validate_exclusive_scope/1` below).\n80\t  \"\"\"\n81\t  @spec start_session(map()) ::\n82\t          {:ok, map()} | {:error, :conflict, String.t()} | {:error, :invalid_params, String.t()}\n83\t  def start_session(params) when is_map(params) do\n84\t    with :ok <- validate_required(params) do\n85\t      case fetch_active() do\n86\t        nil ->\n87\t          insert_session(params)\n88\t\n89\t        existing ->\n90\t          if same_session?(existing, params) do\n91\t            {:ok, existing}\n92\t          else\n93\t            {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n94\t          end\n95\t      end\n96\t    end\n97\t  end\n98\t\n99\t  @doc \"\"\"\n100\t  Manually ends session `id` (AC6): sets `ended_at = now()`, `ended_reason\n101\t  = \"manual_stop\"`.\n102\t\n103\t  Returns:\n104\t\n105\t    * `:ok` — the session was active and is now ended.\n106\t    * `{:error, :not_found}` — no session with this `id` exists.\n107\t    * `{:error, :already_ended}` — the session exists but was already\n108\t      ended (by budget/duration/a previous manual stop) — a safe, clearly\n109\t      defined no-op, never a crash.\n110\t  \"\"\"\n111\t  @spec stop_session(String.t()) :: :ok | {:error, :not_found | :already_ended}\n112\t  def stop_session(id) do\n113\t    result =\n114\t      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n115\t        Nebu.Repo,\n116\t        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' \" <>\n117\t          \"WHERE id = $1 AND ended_at IS NULL\",\n118\t        [id]\n119\t      )\n120\t\n121\t    case result.num_rows do\n122\t      1 ->\n123\t        notify_war_room_reload()\n124\t        :ok\n125\t\n126\t      0 ->\n127\t        case fetch_by_id(id) do\n128\t          nil -> {:error, :not_found}\n129\t          _existing -> {:error, :already_ended}\n130\t        end\n131\t    end\n132\t  end\n133\t\n134\t  @doc \"\"\"\n135\t  Returns `{:ok, session}` for the currently active session, or `:none`\n136\t  when no session is active.\n137\t  \"\"\"\n138\t  @spec status() :: {:ok, map()} | :none\n139\t  def status do\n140\t    case fetch_active() do\n141\t      nil -> :none\n142\t      session -> {:ok, session}\n143\t    end\n144\t  end\n145\t\n146\t  # ── validation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n147\t\n148\t  defp Safe no-op when the process\n370\t  # is not currently running.\n371\t  defp notify_war_room_reload do\n372\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom) do\n373\t      Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active()\n374\t    end\n375\t\n376\t    :ok\n377\t  end\n378\tend\n379\t"}]},"uuid":"603da1e4-36c3-4f99-9946-1ec85ff9bb51","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:19:38.621Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Public API for War Room session lifecycle (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode,\n  AC1/AC2/AC3/AC6/AC10) — `start_session/1`, `stop_session/1`, `status/0`.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n  Design decisions (see #6 for why there is deliberately NO HTTP\n  controller/route here):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n\n  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — no\n  `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge at all, directly unit-testable without an\n  HTTP round trip. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `handle_event(\"start_war_room\" /\n  \"stop_war_room\", ...)` clauses call straight into these functions.\n\n  ## AC2 — \"exactly one active session\"\n\n  This module performs the app-level \"is one already active?\" check first\n  (to return a friendly, human-readable conflict message naming the\n  existing session's `started_by`/`reason`), but the REAL correctness\n  backstop is the migration's partial unique index\n  (`war_room_sessions_single_active_idx`) — a genuinely raced concurrent\n  INSERT still fails there and is mapped to the same `{:error, :conflict,\n  _}` shape.\n\n  Restarting the SAME session (identical `started_by`/`reason`/`scope`/\n  `filter`/`max_entries`/`max_duration_s`) while it is still active is\n  treated as idempotent: `{:ok, existing_session}` is returned without a\n  second row being created.\n\n  ## Cache invalidation\n\n  Every write here (`start_session/1`'s successful insert, `stop_session/1`)\n  calls `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` afterward — mirroring\n  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0` —\n  so the hot-path cache (used by `Handler.log/2` via `WarRoom.check/1`)\n  reflects the change on the very next log entry, not after some future\n  poll.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  @required_params [:started_by, :reason, :max_entries, :max_duration_s]\n\n  # Security review (Kassandra, 2026-07-06, F-1 MEDIUM): these are the SAME\n  # bounds `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `parse_int_bounded/5` already enforces at\n  # the UI layer (AC8/AC11) — duplicated here so the actual trust boundary\n  # (this module's public API, the only thing a caller MUST go through to\n  # create a session) enforces the flooding-safety invariant regardless of\n  # caller, not only when going through the LiveView form. This is\n  # defense-in-depth, the same pattern already used for AC2's \"exactly one\n  # active\" (app-level check + DB-level partial unique index backstop) — the\n  # LiveView's own bounds check stays in place as the friendly\n  # early-UI-feedback layer and is NOT removed/weakened by this.\n  @max_entries_bounds {10, 10_000}\n  @max_duration_s_bounds {10, 3_600}\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Starts a new War Room session with `params` (a map with atom keys):\n\n    * required: `:started_by`, `:reason`, `:max_entries`, `:max_duration_s`\n    * optional: `:scope` (defaults to `nil`, treated as \"all\"), `:filter`\n      (a map, e.g. `%{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}`, defaults to `nil`),\n      `:exclusive_scope` (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page, AC9/AC10\n      — a list of route_template strings to EXCLUDE from capture, defaults\n      to `nil`)\n\n  Returns:\n\n    * `{:ok, session}` — a freshly created session, OR the existing active\n      session when `params` exactly matches it (idempotent restart, AC2).\n    * `{:error, :conflict, message}` — a DIFFERENT session is already\n      active (AC2).\n    * `{:error, :invalid_params, message}` — a required parameter is\n      missing (AC3 — no acceptance test pins the exact shape of this error;\n      this is the chosen, documented contract), OR `:max_entries`/\n      `:max_duration_s` is outside the AC8/AC11 bounds (`10..10_000` /\n      `10..3_600` — security review F-1: this is the actual trust boundary,\n      not just the LiveView form's own bounds check), OR `:exclusive_scope`\n      fails its defense-in-depth shape validation (see\n      `validate_exclusive_scope/1` below).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec start_session(map()) ::\n          {:ok, map()} | {:error, :conflict, String.t()} | {:error, :invalid_params, String.t()}\n  def start_session(params) when is_map(params) do\n    with :ok <- validate_required(params) do\n      case fetch_active() do\n        nil ->\n          insert_session(params)\n\n        existing ->\n          if same_session?(existing, params) do\n            {:ok, existing}\n          else\n            {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n          end\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Manually ends session `id` (AC6): sets `ended_at = now()`, `ended_reason\n  = \"manual_stop\"`.\n\n  Returns:\n\n    * `:ok` — the session was active and is now ended.\n    * `{:error, :not_found}` — no session with this `id` exists.\n    * `{:error, :already_ended}` — the session exists but was already\n      ended (by budget/duration/a previous manual stop) — a safe, clearly\n      defined no-op, never a crash.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec stop_session(String.t()) :: :ok | {:error, :not_found | :already_ended}\n  def stop_session(id) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' \" <>\n          \"WHERE id = $1 AND ended_at IS NULL\",\n        [id]\n      )\n\n    case result.num_rows do\n      1 ->\n        notify_war_room_reload()\n        :ok\n\n      0 ->\n        case fetch_by_id(id) do\n          nil -> {:error, :not_found}\n          _existing -> {:error, :already_ended}\n        end\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Returns `{:ok, session}` for the currently active session, or `:none`\n  when no session is active.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec status() :: {:ok, map()} | :none\n  def status do\n    case fetch_active() do\n      nil -> :none\n      session -> {:ok, session}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ── validation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp validate_required(params) do\n    missing = Enum.filter(@required_params, fn key -> blank?(Map.get(params, key)) end)\n\n    if missing == [] do\n      validate_bounds(params)\n    else\n      {:error, :invalid_params,\n       \"missing required war room parameter(s): #{Enum.map_join(missing, \", \", &to_string/1)}\"}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (AC9/AC10, Dev Notes item 7 —\n  # defense-in-depth): the LiveView form's own checkbox/free-text \"other\"\n  # input is NOT the trust boundary — a caller invoking `start_session/1`\n  # directly (bypassing the form entirely) must still be rejected for a\n  # malformed `exclusive_scope`. AC9 explicitly allows a free-text \"other\"\n  # custom route (not just the canonical `long_polling_endpoints/0`\n  # members), so this is SHAPE validation (every element is a non-empty\n  # string starting with \"/\"), not a closed allowlist — every member of\n  # `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0` always passes\n  # this check, without needing to special-case it.\n  defp validate_exclusive_scope(nil), do: :ok\n\n  defp validate_exclusive_scope(list) when is_list(list) do\n    if Enum.all?(list, &valid_route_shape?/1) do\n      :ok\n    else\n      {:error, :invalid_params,\n       \"exclusive_scope must be a list of non-empty route paths starting with \\\"/\\\" \" <>\n         \"(got #{inspect(list)})\"}\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp validate_exclusive_scope(other) do\n    {:error, :invalid_params, \"exclusive_scope must be a list, got #{inspect(other)}\"}\n  end\n\n  defp valid_route_shape?(value) when is_binary(value), do: String.starts_with?(value, \"/\")\n  defp valid_route_shape?(_value), do: false\n\n  defp blank?(nil), do: true\n  defp blank?(\"\"), do: true\n  defp blank?(_), do: false\n\n  # Security review (Kassandra, F-1 MEDIUM): the actual trust-boundary bounds\n  # check — see the @max_entries_bounds/@max_duration_s_bounds moduledoc\n  # comment above. Runs AFTER required-param presence validation (so a\n  # missing param still reports the more specific \"missing\" error first).\n  defp validate_bounds(params) do\n    {min_entries, max_entries_bound} = @max_entries_bounds\n    {min_duration, max_duration_bound} = @max_duration_s_bounds\n\n    with :ok <-\n           validate_in_range(\n             Map.fetch!(params, :max_entries),\n             \"max_entries\",\n             min_entries,\n             max_entries_bound\n           ),\n         :ok <-\n           validate_in_range(\n             Map.fetch!(params, :max_duration_s),\n             \"max_duration_s\",\n             min_duration,\n             max_duration_bound\n           ),\n         :ok <- validate_exclusive_scope(Map.get(params, :exclusive_scope)) do\n      :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp validate_in_range(value, _field_name, min, max)\n       when is_integer(value) and value >= min and value <= max do\n    :ok\n  end\n\n  defp validate_in_range(value, field_name, min, max) do\n    {:error, :invalid_params,\n     \"#{field_name} must be an integer between #{min} and #{max} (got #{inspect(value)})\"}\n  end\n\n  # ── writes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp insert_session(params) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO war_room_sessions\n          (started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries, max_duration_s)\n        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)\n        RETURNING id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries,\n                  max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        \"\"\",\n        [\n          Map.fetch!(params, :started_by),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :reason),\n          Map.get(params, :scope),\n          Map.get(params, :filter),\n          Map.get(params, :exclusive_scope),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :max_entries),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :max_duration_s)\n        ]\n      )\n\n    [row] = result.rows\n    session = row_to_session(row)\n    notify_war_room_reload()\n    {:ok, session}\n  rescue\n    e in Postgrex.Error ->\n      if unique_violation?(e) do\n        # A genuinely raced concurrent start won this INSERT before we did —\n        # the app-level \"is one active?\" check above already passed, but\n        # the DB-level partial unique index is the real backstop\n        # (design-decisions.md #8).\n        case fetch_active() do\n          nil -> {:error, :conflict, \"War Room already active — could not start a new session\"}\n          existing -> {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n        end\n      else\n        reraise e, __STACKTRACE__\n      end\n  end\n\n  defp unique_violation?(%Postgrex.Error{postgres: %{code: :unique_violation}}), do: true\n  defp unique_violation?(_), do: false\n\n  # ── idempotent-restart comparison (AC2) ─────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp same_session?(existing, params) do\n    existing.started_by == Map.get(params, :started_by) and\n      existing.reason == Map.get(params, :reason) and\n      existing.scope == Map.get(params, :scope) and\n      existing.filter == Map.get(params, :filter) and\n      existing.exclusive_scope == Map.get(params, :exclusive_scope) and\n      existing.max_entries == Map.get(params, :max_entries) and\n      existing.max_duration_s == Map.get(params, :max_duration_s)\n  end\n\n  defp conflict_message(existing) do\n    \"War Room already active, started by #{existing.started_by} at #{existing.started_at}, \" <>\n      \"reason: #{existing.reason} — end it first\"\n  end\n\n  # ── reads ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp fetch_active do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries,\n               max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        FROM war_room_sessions\n        WHERE ended_at IS NULL\n        LIMIT 1\n        \"\"\",\n        []\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n      [] -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp fetch_by_id(id) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries,\n               max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        FROM war_room_sessions\n        WHERE id = $1\n        \"\"\",\n        [id]\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n      [] -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp row_to_session([\n         id,\n         started_by,\n         reason,\n         scope,\n         filter,\n         exclusive_scope,\n         max_entries,\n         max_duration_s,\n         started_at,\n         ended_at,\n         ended_reason,\n         entries_collected\n       ]) do\n    %{\n      id: id,\n      started_by: started_by,\n      reason: reason,\n      scope: scope,\n      filter: filter,\n      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n      max_entries: max_entries,\n      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n      started_at: started_at,\n      ended_at: ended_at,\n      ended_reason: ended_reason,\n      entries_collected: entries_collected\n    }\n  end\n\n  # Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Shared helper for the war-room recording dot/pill assigns\n  (`:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/`:war_room_started_by`)\n  threaded through `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (enhanced-logging/\n  war-room-dedicated-page, amendment 4).\n\n  Every remaining admin LiveView (`Nebu.Admin.DashboardLive`,\n  `Nebu.Admin.AuditLogLive`, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`,\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` — the only\n  LiveViews left after admin-http-refactor's controller conversions, i.e.\n  the full \"anywhere in the admin UI\" surface) calls `status/0` at mount and\n  on its own 2s `schedule/0` tick, then `assign_status/2` to set these three\n  assigns. Polling is UNCONDITIONAL once connected (not gated on \"already\n  active\") — the dot/pill must be able to APPEAR later, while the operator\n  is already on an unrelated page, not merely persist while already\n  showing (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own pre-existing\n  \"always rescheduled... because the banner must also APPEAR when a session\n  starts later\" war-room poll, generalized here to every remaining\n  LiveView).\n  \"\"\"\n  import Phoenix.Component, only: [assign: 3]\n\n  @refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n  @refresh_message :refresh_war_room_status\n\n  @doc \"The message name `handle_info/2` should match on for the scheduled refresh tick.\"\n  @spec refresh_message() :: atom()\n  def refresh_message, do: @refresh_message\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  A live DB read of the current War Room status — `:none` when\n  `Nebu.Repo` is not running (plain unit-test runs), mirroring every other\n  DB-guarded helper in this codebase.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec status() :: {:ok, map()} | :none\n  def status do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status()\n    else\n      :none\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"Schedules the next refresh tick (`schedule/0`'s own `refresh_message/0`, 2s).\"\n  @spec schedule() :: reference()\n  def schedule, do: Process.send_after(self(), @refresh_message, @refresh_interval_ms)\n\n  @doc \"Assigns `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/`:war_room_started_by` from `status`.\"\n  @spec assign_status(Phoenix.LiveView.Socket.t(), {:ok, map()} | :none) ::\n          Phoenix.LiveView.Socket.t()\n  def assign_status(socket, {:ok, session}) do\n    socket\n    |> assign(:war_room_active, true)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_at, session.started_at)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_by, session.started_by)\n  end\n\n  def assign_status(socket, :none) do\n    socket\n    |> assign(:war_room_active, false)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_at, nil)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_by, nil)\n  end\nend\n","numLines":66,"startLine":1,"totalLines":66}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"561db368-d271-4a5a-828c-fd51d69b3a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"33dfb2e6-e15d-4933-a4b4-b2e47f8507af","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HiUHYA7wDcGetzoLZDRatz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t-- enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (AC9/AC10, Dev Notes Option A —\n2\t-- \"recommended\"): the \"exclusive scope\" convenience syntax (\"capture\n3\t-- everything EXCEPT these endpoints\", primarily motivated by\n4\t-- `/_matrix/client/v3/sync` dominating any endpoint-scoped capture).\n5\t--\n6\t-- Kept as a SEPARATE, nullable JSONB column from `scope` (which stays a\n7\t-- plain string: NULL/\"all\"/a specific route_template, unchanged from\n8\t-- 000019) rather than refactoring `scope` into a JSONB map — minimal\n9\t-- change, backward-compatible with every pre-existing\n10\t-- `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom`/`Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` code path that\n11\t-- reads `scope` as a string.\n12\t--\n13\t-- NULL or an empty JSON array is a pure no-op (100% backward compatible —\n14\t-- see `war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs`). A non-empty JSON array of\n15\t-- route_template strings means: an entry whose route_template is a member\n16\t-- of this list is NEVER tagged/counted, regardless of what `scope`/`filter`\n17\t-- would otherwise have matched.\n18\tALTER TABLE war_room_sessions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS exclusive_scope JSONB;\n19\t"}]},"uuid":"9811b1fc-5372-4ced-a300-73ccda76fa70","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:19:40.132Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/priv/migrations/000021_war_room_exclusive_scope.up.sql","content":"-- enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (AC9/AC10, Dev Notes Option A —\n-- \"recommended\"): the \"exclusive scope\" convenience syntax (\"capture\n-- everything EXCEPT these endpoints\", primarily motivated by\n-- `/_matrix/client/v3/sync` dominating any endpoint-scoped capture).\n--\n-- Kept as a SEPARATE, nullable JSONB column from `scope` (which stays a\n-- plain string: NULL/\"all\"/a specific route_template, unchanged from\n-- 000019) rather than refactoring `scope` into a JSONB map — minimal\n-- change, backward-compatible with every pre-existing\n-- `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom`/`Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` code path that\n-- reads `scope` as a string.\n--\n-- NULL or an empty JSON array is a pure no-op (100% backward compatible —\n-- see `war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs`). A non-empty JSON array of\n-- route_template strings means: an entry whose route_template is a member\n-- of this list is NEVER tagged/counted, regardless of what `scope`/`filter`\n-- would otherwise have matched.\nALTER TABLE war_room_sessions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS exclusive_scope JSONB;\n","numLines":19,"startLine":1,"totalLines":19}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"33dfb2e6-e15d-4933-a4b4-b2e47f8507af","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
