{"parentUuid":"bf082a64-536d-4197-9580-b3be6e2766e8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011vZxssktZKkasx3LRcPVpD","type":"tool_result","content":"175\t  options map supporting `level`, `source`, `route_template`, `request_id` —\n176\t  any subset. An empty/no-filter map returns every row, most-recent first.\n177\t  \"\"\"\n178\t  @spec get_logs(map()) :: {:ok, [map()]}\n179\t  def get_logs(filters) when is_map(filters) do\n180\t    {where_sql, params} = build_where(filters)\n181\t\n182\t    sql = \"\"\"\n183\t    SELECT id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id\n184\t    FROM operational_logs\n185\t    #{where_sql}\n186\t    ORDER BY inserted_at DESC\n187\t    \"\"\"\n188\t\n189\t    result = Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, sql, params)\n190\t    {:ok, Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_log/1)}\n191\t  end\n192\t\n193\t  # ── policy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n194\t\n195\t  defp read_server_config do\n196\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n197\t      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n198\t      config\n199\t    else\n200\t      %{}\n201\t    end\n202\t  end\n203\t\n204\t  defp to_bool(nil), do: false\n205\t  defp to_bool(true), do: true\n206\t  defp to_bool(false), do: false\n207\t  defp to_bool(\"true\"), do: true\n208\t  defp to_bool(\"false\"), do: false\n209\t  defp to_bool(_), do: false\n210\t\n211\t  defp to_int(nil), do: nil\n212\t  defp to_int(value) when is_integer(value), do: value\n213\t\n214\t  defp to_int(value) when is_binary(value) do\n215\t    case Integer.parse(value) do\n216\t      {int, _rest} -> int\n217\t      :error -> nil\n218\t    end\n219\t  end\n220\t\n221\t  defp to_int(_), do: nil\n222\t\n223\t  # ── get_logs/1 helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n224\t\n225\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): war_room_id is already SELECTed\n226\t  # and returned by row_to_log/1 below — it was just missing from this\n227\t  # allowlist, making the AC12 \"filter the stream by war_room_id\" use case\n228\t  # silently impossible (the filter key was accepted but ignored) even\n229\t  # though the column/SELECT already supported it.\n230\t  @allowed_filter_keys ~w(level source route_template request_id war_room_id)\n231\t\n232\t  defp build_where(filters) do\n233\t    filters\n234\t    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), v} end)\n235\t    |> Enum.filter(fn {k, _v} -> k in @allowed_filter_keys end)\n236\t    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {k, normalize_filter_value(k, v)} end)\n237\t    |> Enum.with_index(1)\n238\t    |> Enum.reduce({[], []}, fn {{k, v}, idx}, {conds, params} ->\n239\t      {conds ++ [\"#{k} = $#{idx}\"], params ++ [v]}\n240\t    end)\n241\t    |> then(fn {conds, params} ->\n242\t      where_sql = if conds == [], do: \"\", else: \"WHERE \" <> Enum.join(conds, \" AND \")\n243\t      {where_sql, params}\n244\t    end)\n245\t  end\n246\t\n247\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): `war_room_id` is a native\n248\t  # Postgres `uuid` column — Postgrex decodes/encodes `uuid` as a 16-byte\n249\t  # BINARY (see `Postgrex.Extensions.UUID`), unlike every other identifier\n250\t  # in this codebase (room_id/user_id/device_id/request_id), which are\n251\t  # plain TEXT and therefore always the human-readable string form.\n252\t  # `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.mount/3`'s `war_room_id` URL query param arrives\n253\t  # as a canonical hex-dash STRING (as any real bookmarked URL would carry\n254\t  # it); a value obtained directly from a prior `RETURNING id`/`SELECT id`\n255\t  # call elsewhere may already be the raw 16-byte binary form. Accept\n256\t  # EITHER here and normalize to whichever form Postgrex's own encoder\n257\t  # actually requires for a `uuid`-typed bound parameter (raw binary) —\n258\t  # `Ecto.UUID.cast/1` accepts both forms as input, `Ecto.UUID.dump!/1`\n259\t  # then converts the resulting canonical string to that raw binary.\n260\t  defp normalize_filter_value(\"war_room_id\", v) when is_binary(v) do\n261\t    case Ecto.UUID.cast(v) do\n262\t      {:ok, canonical_string} -> Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical_string)\n263\t      :error -> v\n264\t    end\n265\t  end\n266\t\n267\t  defp normalize_filter_value(_key, v), do: v\n268\t\n269\t  defp row_to_log([id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id]) do\n270\t    %{\n271\t      id: id,\n272\t      inserted_at: inserted_at,\n273\t      level: level,\n274\t      source: source,\n275\t      route_template: route_template,\n276\t      request_id: request_id,\n277\t      message: message,\n278\t      metadata: metadata,\n279\t      war_room_id: war_room_id\n280\t    }\n281\t  end\n282\tend\n283\t"}]},"uuid":"07e38cbb-a0c4-47a6-8ccc-a9e96a681259","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:20:12.968Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store.ex","content":"  options map supporting `level`, `source`, `route_template`, `request_id` —\n  any subset. An empty/no-filter map returns every row, most-recent first.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec get_logs(map()) :: {:ok, [map()]}\n  def get_logs(filters) when is_map(filters) do\n    {where_sql, params} = build_where(filters)\n\n    sql = \"\"\"\n    SELECT id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id\n    FROM operational_logs\n    #{where_sql}\n    ORDER BY inserted_at DESC\n    \"\"\"\n\n    result = Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, sql, params)\n    {:ok, Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_log/1)}\n  end\n\n  # ── policy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp read_server_config do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n      config\n    else\n      %{}\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp to_bool(nil), do: false\n  defp to_bool(true), do: true\n  defp to_bool(false), do: false\n  defp to_bool(\"true\"), do: true\n  defp to_bool(\"false\"), do: false\n  defp to_bool(_), do: false\n\n  defp to_int(nil), do: nil\n  defp to_int(value) when is_integer(value), do: value\n\n  defp to_int(value) when is_binary(value) do\n    case Integer.parse(value) do\n      {int, _rest} -> int\n      :error -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp to_int(_), do: nil\n\n  # ── get_logs/1 helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): war_room_id is already SELECTed\n  # and returned by row_to_log/1 below — it was just missing from this\n  # allowlist, making the AC12 \"filter the stream by war_room_id\" use case\n  # silently impossible (the filter key was accepted but ignored) even\n  # though the column/SELECT already supported it.\n  @allowed_filter_keys ~w(level source route_template request_id war_room_id)\n\n  defp build_where(filters) do\n    filters\n    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), v} end)\n    |> Enum.filter(fn {k, _v} -> k in @allowed_filter_keys end)\n    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {k, normalize_filter_value(k, v)} end)\n    |> Enum.with_index(1)\n    |> Enum.reduce({[], []}, fn {{k, v}, idx}, {conds, params} ->\n      {conds ++ [\"#{k} = $#{idx}\"], params ++ [v]}\n    end)\n    |> then(fn {conds, params} ->\n      where_sql = if conds == [], do: \"\", else: \"WHERE \" <> Enum.join(conds, \" AND \")\n      {where_sql, params}\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): `war_room_id` is a native\n  # Postgres `uuid` column — Postgrex decodes/encodes `uuid` as a 16-byte\n  # BINARY (see `Postgrex.Extensions.UUID`), unlike every other identifier\n  # in this codebase (room_id/user_id/device_id/request_id), which are\n  # plain TEXT and therefore always the human-readable string form.\n  # `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.mount/3`'s `war_room_id` URL query param arrives\n  # as a canonical hex-dash STRING (as any real bookmarked URL would carry\n  # it); a value obtained directly from a prior `RETURNING id`/`SELECT id`\n  # call elsewhere may already be the raw 16-byte binary form. Accept\n  # EITHER here and normalize to whichever form Postgrex's own encoder\n  # actually requires for a `uuid`-typed bound parameter (raw binary) —\n  # `Ecto.UUID.cast/1` accepts both forms as input, `Ecto.UUID.dump!/1`\n  # then converts the resulting canonical string to that raw binary.\n  defp normalize_filter_value(\"war_room_id\", v) when is_binary(v) do\n    case Ecto.UUID.cast(v) do\n      {:ok, canonical_string} -> Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical_string)\n      :error -> v\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_filter_value(_key, v), do: v\n\n  defp row_to_log([id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id]) do\n    %{\n      id: id,\n      inserted_at: inserted_at,\n      level: level,\n      source: source,\n      route_template: route_template,\n      request_id: request_id,\n      message: message,\n      metadata: metadata,\n      war_room_id: war_room_id\n    }\n  end\nend\n","numLines":109,"startLine":175,"totalLines":283}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bf082a64-536d-4197-9580-b3be6e2766e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"c819ec65-a9a4-476b-99e2-5643ac306a59","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00703992-e59f-41eb-8bab-77b50e50da12","agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Qa5mE9p7y8ib2bgFXxTskk","type":"tool_result","content":"  The config-driven off-by-default long-polling endpoint list (AC4/AC13).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec long_polling_endpoints() :: [String.t()]\n  def long_polling_endpoints, do: @long_polling_endpoints\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Whether `route` is one of the off-by-default long-polling endpoints\n  (AC4). `nil`/non-binary routes are never off-tier.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec off_endpoint?(term()) :: boolean()\n  def off_endpoint?(route) when is_binary(route), do: route in @long_polling_endpoints\n  def off_endpoint?(_route), do: false\n\n=== xss test ===\n  describe \"security: an admin-supplied exclusive_other value with HTML-special characters is escaped on render\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n      on_exit(fn ->\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"DELETE FROM war_room_sessions WHERE started_by LIKE 'distinctive-wr-page-test-%'\",\n          []\n        )\n      end)\n\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"a route-shaped exclusive_other value containing raw HTML tags is never emitted unescaped on the detail page\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_list()\n      reason = new_started_by()\n      malicious = \"/admin/<b>xss-probe</b>\"\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"]))\n      |> render_submit(%{\n        \"reason\" => reason,\n        \"scope\" => \"exclusive\",\n        \"exclusive_scope\" => [],\n        \"exclusive_other\" => malicious,\n        \"user_id\" => \"\",\n        \"device_id\" => \"\",\n        \"max_entries\" => \"100\",\n        \"max_duration_s\" => \"300\"\n      })\n\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"SELECT id FROM war_room_sessions WHERE reason = $1\",\n          [reason]\n        )\n\n      assert [[id]] = result.rows,\n             \"precondition: the route-shaped value must pass shape validation and persist \" <>\n               \"(this test is about RENDER-time escaping, not input rejection)\"\n\n      {:ok, _detail_view, detail_html} = mount_via_router(\"/admin/logs/war-room/#{id}\")\n\n      refute detail_html =~ malicious,\n             \"security (security_review: required — Kassandra focus area): the raw, \" <>\n               \"unescaped exclusive_other value must never appear verbatim in the detail \" <>\n               \"page's HTML. An admin-supplied route string is untrusted input; shape \" <>\n               \"validation alone (must start with \\\"/\\\") does not exclude HTML-special \" <>\n               \"characters, so escaping at RENDER time is the real defense here\"\n\n      assert detail_html =~ \"&lt;b&gt;xss-probe&lt;/b&gt;\" or\n               (parsed(detail_html)\n                |> find(~s([data-testid=\"war-room-detail-meta\"]))\n                |> Floki.text() =~ \"xss-probe\"),\n             \"expected the value to still be DISPLAYED, as inert escaped text (not silently \" <>\n               \"stripped) — proves the fix is escaping, not merely deleting the field: \" <>\n               \"got #{inspect(detail_html)}\"\n    end\n  end\nend","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"c4e743aa-17fe-46f1-bb0a-a058423b0b42","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:23:32.284Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"  The config-driven off-by-default long-polling endpoint long-polling endpoints\n  (AC4). `nil`/non-binary routes are never off-tier.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec off_endpoint?(term()) :: boolean()\n  def off_endpoint?(route) when is_binary(route), do: route in @long_polling_endpoints\n  def off_endpoint?(_route), do: false\n\n=== xss test ===\n  describe \"security: an admin-supplied exclusive_other value with HTML-special characters is escaped on render\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n      on_exit(fn ->\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"DELETE FROM HTML tags is never emitted unescaped on the detail page\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_list()\n      reason = new_started_by()\n      malicious = \"/admin/<b>xss-probe</b>\"\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"]))\n      |> render_submit(%{\n        \"reason\" => reason,\n        \"scope\" => \"exclusive\",\n Pattern seen in war-room-dedicated-page\n(`LogsLive.WarRoom.start_war_room`): scope=\"exclusive\" with no checkboxes and\nblank \"other\" -> `merge_exclusive_scope/1` returns `nil` -> stored as\n`scope:\"all\", exclusive_scope:nil` = capture EVERYTHING. {"parentUuid":"8e2dc935-173a-4142-960a-d5283240f409","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"addeae4ab9c5c2cba","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Ccpk1eaM8xa7utimsn499","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Code review complete for `war-room-dedicated-page`.\n\n---\n\n**0 MAJOR, 1 MINOR, 4 INFO**\n\n## Verdict\nNo MAJOR/CRITICAL issues. Implementation is correct against all numbered ACs and the authoritative 2026-07-08 amendments, ADR-018/019/020/021 compliant, cluster-safe, and the XSS/escaping surface Kassandra will look at is already clean and test-pinned. One MINOR (opt-out edge case) to hand back to the dev agent; four INFO notes for the record.\n\n---\n\n## Review dimensions — findings\n\n### 1. Correctness — PASS (one MINOR)\n- **exclusive_scope precedence in `WarRoom.check/1`** — correct AND semantics. `matches?/2` composes as `not excluded?(...) and scope_matches?(...) and filter_matches?(...)` (`server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/war_room.ex:240-244`). An excluded `route_template` is skipped even when `scope:\"all\"` and the metadata filter would otherwise match — exactly AC10's \"if yes, skip: do not tag, do not count\". No accidental OR. `nil`/`[]` exclusive_scope is a pure no-op (`excluded?/2` clauses 246-247). JSONB decodes to a native list, consumed safely by `route in exclusive_scope`.\n- **Shape-validation posture in `WarRoomManager.start_session/1`** — matches the story's explicit \"shape-only, not closed allowlist\" decision. `validate_exclusive_scope/1` (`server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex:169-186`) requires every element to be a non-empty binary starting with `/`. Cannot be bypassed to write non-path garbage that breaks `check/1` downstream — non-binaries/non-lists are rejected with `:invalid_params`, and any list that passes is safe for `route in list`. Runs at the real trust boundary (the public API), not just the form.\n- **copy-to-markdown template** — matches Dev Notes format exactly (`markdown_text/2`, `markdown_entry_line/1`, `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex:548-567`).\n- **Detail-view log filtering** — `load_entries/1` uses `get_logs(%{war_room_id: room_id})`; only that session's entries, reversed to ascending chronological order (AC5). Test at `war_room_test.exs:560` asserts entries from a different session are excluded.\n- **404 for malformed/non-existent room_id** — `load_session/1` casts via `Ecto.UUID.cast |> dump`, returns `nil` on `:error`, and is wrapped in a `rescue` that maps any crash to `nil` → `war-room-not-found` render. A non-UUID URL gracefully 404s, does not crash (test `war_room_test.exs:648`).\n- **MINOR** — see triage below (exclusive mode with empty selection silently captures all).\n\n### 2. Cluster-safety (ADR-019) — PASS\n`WarRoomIndicator.status/0` (`server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_indicator.ex:37-43`) does a fresh `WarRoomManager.status/0` DB read on every 2s tick — no caching, no node-local ownership, no `persistent_term`. All nodes converge on the DB (single source of truth) within one poll interval, so the sidebar dot/topbar pill are correct cross-node. No new process ownership introduced. The pre-existing node-local `persistent_term` hot-path flag in `Web.LogStore.WarRoom` is untouched by this story.\n\n### 3. Public-API / ADR-018/020/021 — PASS\nAdmin → `WarRoomManager` is intra-Admin. Admin → `Web.LogStore` (`get_logs/1`) is the same shared-infrastructure path `LogsLive` already uses (pre-existing, not a new cross-domain hop). All SQL is inline in the owning module (no repository/service layer). Migration 000021 is a plain, idempotent, backward-compatible `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ... JSONB` (Option A per Dev Notes) with a matching `DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS` down.\n\n### 4. HTML-escaping / XSS — PASS (nothing for Kassandra to find here)\nEvery admin/operator-supplied field (`reason`, `filter`, `scope_label`, `exclusive_scope`, `exclusive_other`, and `data-copy-text` markdown) is rendered through ordinary HEEx `{...}` interpolation, auto-escaped including in attribute position. No `raw/1`/`Phoenix.HTML.raw/1`/`safe_html` anywhere in the module. Directly pinned by `war_room_test.exs:1026` — a route-shaped `exclusive_other` of `/admin/<b>xss-probe</b>` persists (shape-valid) yet is emitted only as escaped `&lt;b&gt;...`. (Message-content redaction on the detail stream remains Kassandra's amendment-7 call, but it's already handled upstream in `RequestLogger`, consistent with the Leitstand panel.)\n\n### 5. Scope-creep check — JUSTIFIED, not over-broad\n`dashboard_live.ex` and `audit_log_live.ex` changes are amendment-4's \"anywhere in the admin UI\" requirement, not creep. `WarRoomIndicator`'s moduledoc enumerates the complete remaining LiveView set after admin-http-refactor's controller conversions: `DashboardLive`, `AuditLogLive`, `LogsLive`, `LogsLive.TraceMap`, `LogsLive.WarRoom`. Touching exactly these five is the minimum to satisfy \"visible from any admin page\" — the other admin surfaces are now plain HTTP controllers with no socket to poll. Each site does identical minimal wiring (schedule at mount + `:refresh_war_room_status` handler + `assign_status`). Correct and proportionate.\n\n### 6. Performance — acceptable (same class previously accepted; see INFO)\nThe form's live preview is unthrottled (each `operational_logs` PubSub entry re-renders the level/source bubbles). This is the same render-cost class code-review already asked-and-accepted for the trace-map page, and the preview payload is tiny (7 bubble counts). Not a blocker. Two INFO notes below.\n\n### 7. Test quality on the dev's own fixes — PASS\nCycle fixes are covered: UUID canonicalization is regression-pinned by the detail-view tests (`war_room_test.exs:560`, `:648`); exclusive_scope has dedicated files (`war_room_manager_exclusive_scope_test.exs`, `log_store/war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs`); `:readonly` mode (`trace_map_bubble_readonly_mode_test.exs`); indicator wiring (`layouts_war_room_indicator_test.exs`); panel retirement (`war_room_panel_retirement_test.exs`). The E2E-only fixes (db.ts, `phx-connected` waits) aren't unit-testable but are exercised by the 10/10 story E2E suite under Before-hook isolation. No ad-hoc/unpinned fixes — the recurring \"fix without regression test\" pattern does **not** recur here.\n\n---\n\n## Triage\n\n### MINOR (hand back to dev agent)\n1. **Exclusive scope selected with no endpoints checked/entered silently captures everything.** In `handle_event(\"start_war_room\", ...)` (`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex:204-209`), `scope == \"exclusive\"` with no checkboxes and blank \"other\" makes `merge_exclusive_scope/1` return `nil`, stored as `scope:\"all\", exclusive_scope:nil` — i.e. capture-all, the exact noisy full capture the operator chose \"all except…\" to avoid. No validation or warning surfaces. Suggested fix: require ≥1 exclusion when scope is `exclusive`, or show a visible \"excluding nothing = capturing all\" warning.\n\n### INFO (no action mandated)\n2. **Detail view is not live-updating.** `handle_info/2` ignores `operational_logs` entries when `mode == :detail` (`war_room.ex:174-180`), so an active session's detail page won't reflect newly-captured entries without a reload. Original Dev Notes item 4 described a live-updating detail view, but the authoritative amendments don't re-affirm it and no numbered AC or test requires it. Flagging the deliberate simplification for the record.\n3. **Preview counts are cumulative-since-mount, not a rolling \"right now\" window.** `ingest_preview/2` monotonically increments `level_counts`/`source_counts` and never decays/resets (`war_room.ex:328-335`). AC8 wanted \"what traffic looks like right now\"; the bubbles convey relative shape but drift from a true live snapshot the longer the page stays open. Intentional (test `war_room_test.exs:715` pins the live-increment behavior).\n4. **Preview seed does an unbounded full-table read.** `seed_preview_counts/1` calls `get_logs(%{})` (no SQL `LIMIT`) then `Enum.take(rows, 500)` (`war_room.ex:319-326`). This mirrors the pre-existing, accepted `LogsLive`/`TraceMap` pattern (which take 200), so it's consistent — but 500 is 2.5x that cap and `operational_logs` is the epic's highest-volume table. If the accepted pattern is ever revisited for a `LIMIT`-at-SQL fix, include this call site.\n5. **Free-text \"other\" without a leading `/` yields a developer-ish error.** A value like `sync` (no slash) passes `merge_exclusive_scope/1` but is rejected by `start_session/1` with the raw `\"exclusive_scope must be a list of non-empty route paths starting with \\\"/\\\"...\"` message shown in `@start_error`.
